# 251: Chapter 252: Physical Powerhouse!

"Milotic, use Tackle!"

The moment Wallace gave that decisive command, the entire battlefield fell silent.

No one could imagine what kind of suffering Wallace had gone through to resort to having his beloved ace Milotic use Tackle.

Tackle—something every rookie trainer used when they first started—a move that was quickly abandoned as Pokémon grew stronger.

But today, Hoenn's Champion!

The "Artist of Water,"

The trainer who upheld honor and elegance in battle,

The trainer who believed water illusions were the pinnacle of beauty,

Was now, in front of a crowd, using Tackle.

Milotic let out a soft cry, its long, serpentine body flicking forward, glowing faintly with white light as it rushed toward Smeargle.

A Normal-type Tackle!

"Huh? Steven, what are you doing?"

Lillie could barely hold back her laughter, especially after seeing Wallace's determined and solemn expression—it was too much.

But then, from the corner of her eye, she noticed something surprising—

Steven was recording a video on his phone!

"Huh? Oh, well… this battle is just too iconic. It has to be recorded."

Steven blinked, then coughed lightly, trying to look serious.

This was a legendary moment—his best friend's finest hour.

It had to be saved.

"Lillie, Cynthia… I know what you're thinking, but I must do this."

Steven adjusted his phone's angle.

"Steven…" Cynthia fell into deep thought.

"?"

"Send me a copy." Cynthia said seriously.

"Of course! Though I regret not recording from the very start…"

Steven sighed, deeply regretting his mistake. He should have recorded the whole battle!

But then, his sharp eyes scanned the crowd—until he spotted a girl with a camera recording everything.

His eyes lit up as he immediately approached her.

"Excuse me."

"Eh? Oh—Steven!"

The girl turned around, her eyes lighting up when she saw Steven.

"May?"

Steven blinked. He actually recognized her.

On the battlefield…

Seeing Wallace actually make Milotic charge forward, Dylan's smirk grew even wider.

This was hilarious—he was having the time of his life~!

"Wallace, your determination is admirable, but a simple Tackle won't cut it."

Dylan teased.

"Dylan! Don't underestimate my Milotic!" Wallace's beautiful face was dead serious.

"A mere Smeargle—let's see if it can withstand my Milotic's strength head-on!"

Wallace trusted his Milotic completely. Sure, most of its moves had been sealed—which meant it could only use Tackle.

But who cares?!

This was just a Smeargle. Even if Dylan let it use Giga Impact, it still wouldn't overpower Milotic!

"Oh?" Dylan raised an eyebrow.

Smeargle's body flashed white—Moody activated again.

Its Defense increased by two stages, while its Special Attack decreased by one.

Milotic was fast, and it was already closing in on Smeargle.

Compared to Smeargle, Milotic was a giant.

Milotic was gorgeous, but in reality, it was a large-sized Pokémon.

The average Milotic was 6.2 meters long, but Wallace's Milotic—after years of careful training—was at least 6.8 meters.

But size doesn't decide battles.

"Spiky Shield." Dylan commanded calmly.

"!!!" Wallace's pupils shrank.

"Wait—Milotic, stop!"

Grass-type move—Spiky Shield!

Like Baneful Bunker and King's Shield, it was a protective move.

Smeargle swished its tail, summoning a green, spiked barrier around itself.

As Milotic crashed into the shield, sharp green thorns pierced into its body.

Milotic let out a pained cry and immediately recoiled.

Spiky Shield not only blocked attacks but also inflicted damage if the opponent made contact.

Because Milotic had Safeguard active, Dylan couldn't use Fire Pledge or Baneful Bunker—but Spiky Shield still worked!

"…"

Wallace's normally flawless, graceful face was now dark and grim.

He felt… nauseous.

How is this guy even real?!

This battle style—how had no challenger ever smashed his Gym doors down in rage?!

Wallace was now 100% convinced that the high ratings for the Rota Gym were fake.

Dylan was rich—if he wanted to, he could easily fake reviews.

Otherwise, how could anyone survive this nonsense?!

Unless… Kanto trainers were all masochists.

And this was just Dylan's Normal-type team.

What if he used Dark, Ghost, or Poison-types—all known for their toxic playstyles?

Wallace fell into deep, painful silence.

"Dylan, aren't you going to attack?"

Dylan, seeing Wallace frozen in place, asked politely.

Wallace said nothing.

He didn't want to fight anymore.

Dylan's battle tactics were insane.

How was he supposed to win?!

Smeargle sealed all his attacks.

Milotic couldn't even use Water Gun.

Tackle was useless.

And now, it was taking free damage from Spiky Shield.

If battle rules allowed substitutes, Wallace would have begged Steven to take over.

But as Hoenn's Champion, he couldn't just forfeit in front of a crowd—especially after talking so much at the start.

Wallace's silence was deafening.

"Alright, I get it. Smeargle's low offensive stats make it hard to finish off your Pokémon."

Dylan nodded in understanding. Maybe he had gone too far.

But he was very satisfied with Smeargle's performance.

"Let me switch to a more direct fighter."

"Smeargle, use—actually, never mind. Just Parting Shot."

Dylan considered using Baton Pass, but that might ruin Wallace's experience even more.

Smeargle waved its tail like a microphone, roasting Milotic with a nonstop barrage of insults.

Milotic tilted its head in confusion.

A mysterious energy spread outward—Milotic's Attack and Special Attack dropped by one stage.

Then, Smeargle glowed white and disappeared into its Poké Ball.

"!!!" Wallace's eyes shone with newfound hope.

That damn dog was finally gone!

For the first time in this battle, Wallace's entire aura changed.

Vitality. Confidence. Determination!

"Dylan is too kind." Steven sighed.

Cynthia's eyelids twitched. She glanced at Steven.

This man was sighing… because Wallace was no longer suffering?

So he wanted to keep watching Wallace suffer?!

"I wonder which Pokémon Dylan will use next." Cynthia was more curious about Dylan's next choice.

If Dylan wanted a strong physical attacker in his Normal-type team…

"Slaking, let's go!"

Dylan threw out his second Poké Ball.

With a flash of light, a huge, powerful gorilla-like Pokémon landed heavily on the battlefield.

It let out a yawn and stretched lazily.

Slaking—Dylan's main physical powerhouse in his Normal-type team!

[Beep—Slaking, Normal-type, the Lazy Pokémon]Ability: TruantMoves: Hammer Arm, Mega Kick, Counter, Abyssal Thrust, Crush Claw, Sucker Punch, Thunder Punch…

Description: Although it is the laziest Pokémon in the world, it can unleash all of its accumulated energy at once, displaying astonishing power.

Hearing Rotom's Pokédex entry, Cynthia and Steven were certain of one thing—this Slaking was incredibly strong!

"Dylan, I'll make sure to experience your Slaking's strength firsthand."

Dylan smiled as he spoke.

Slaking was the physical powerhouse of his Normal-type team!

"Slaking, huh?"

Wallace pondered for a moment. Slaking shouldn't have any tricky tactics, right?

"Return, Milotic."

Wallace still recalled Milotic, knowing that Slaking—despite its Truant ability—was an extremely formidable opponent.

If Truant wasn't active, a rampaging Slaking could overpower most Pokémon.

"Let's go, Gyarados!"

Wallace sent out Gyarados, testing Slaking's capabilities.

The moment Gyarados appeared, fierce flames surged from its body—it was still burned.

The burns seared through Gyarados, making it groan in pain.

"Charge in!" Dylan commanded without hesitation.

Upon hearing his trainer's command, Slaking's once lazy, drowsy expression sharpened instantly.

With a wild, beast-like roar, it rushed forward on all fours, launching a ferocious charge at Gyarados.

Its brutal, violent aura was overwhelming!

"Gyarados, fly!"

Wallace reacted immediately—he had chosen Gyarados because of its ability to fly.

Gyarados flapped its fins, trying to ascend.

"Grab it."

A deep growl erupted as Slaking's massive hand latched onto Gyarados's tail, preventing it from taking off.

Gyarados looked down—right into Slaking's menacing grin.

A grin that resembled a demon's.

"Gyarados! Water Pulse!"

Wallace quickly ordered an attack.

Gyarados turned its head, gathering blue energy in its mouth—Water Pulse!

"Thunder Punch!"

Slaking growled, its right fist crackling with electricity, and in the shocked eyes of the crowd, it lifted Gyarados into the air—

And slammed it into the battlefield.

BOOM!

The entire arena trembled from the impact, showing just how much power Slaking had put into that slam.

Gyarados let out a painful roar—even though the attack wasn't a registered move, it still hurt— a lot.

Zzzzt!

Crackling lightning surged around Slaking's massive fist, and with a vicious snarl, it swung down—

THUNDER PUNCH!

BOOM!

The electrified fist struck Gyarados's body, causing it to convulse violently.

Gyarados arched in pain, but within seconds, its body slumped to the ground, completely still.

"Gyarados is unable to battle! Slaking wins!"

Cynthia announced the result.

"Whoa!! That was insane!"

"It's mostly because Gyarados was burned—its full power wasn't unleashed."

"But honestly, even if it wasn't burned, I think Slaking still would've won. Wallace is completely outmatched…"

"At this rate, there's no way Wallace can defeat this Gym Leader."

"See? I always said Steven is stronger than Wallace."

"Dylan is incredible!"

The crowd erupted into lively discussions.

After defeating Gyarados, Slaking immediately flopped onto the ground, supporting its head with one arm, and let out a loud yawn.

Its Truant ability had activated.

The terrifying battle-hardened beast from earlier vanished—leaving only a lazy gorilla lounging on the battlefield.

But everyone understood—

This seemingly harmless Slaking harbored a sleeping monster within.

"That Slaking is scary strong!"

A girl in the crowd—May—couldn't help but click her tongue in awe.

Her father, Norman, was known as the strongest Slaking trainer in the world.

May had seen Norman's Slaking fight before, but somehow, this Slaking felt different.

This Slaking's ferocity in battle was overwhelming!

"Slaking's combat ability has always been top-tier. In a direct fight, it's even stronger than pseudo-Legendary Pokémon."

Cynthia furrowed her brows. Slaking's strength was well-known.

Nobody denied how powerful Slaking was.

However, its Truant ability severely held it back—unless in Double Battles, where its ability could be swapped or removed, very few trainers actually used Slaking in serious battles.

After all, having to take a break every other turn was a major drawback.

Wallace frowned deeply before sending out his fourth Pokémon.

A strong, blue Pokémon landed before him—

Hoenn's Water-type starter—Swampert!

(Yes, Wallace had a Swampert in the Black & White 2 Tournament—this isn't made up.)

"Swampert! Hammer Arm!"

Wallace immediately ordered.

Swampert rushed forward, raising its massive arms before bringing them crashing down toward Slaking.

Fighting-type Hammer Arm!

Since Slaking was weak to Fighting-types, this attack was highly effective!

BAM!

Swampert's Hammer Arm slammed into Slaking's body, making it roar in pain—the damage was significant!

"One more Hammer Arm!" Wallace pressed the advantage.

Swampert lifted its arms again, preparing another attack.

"Grab it."

At that moment, a giant hand shot out—clamping onto Swampert's ankle.

Swampert winced—the grip was too tight!

Looking down, Swampert saw Slaking's menacing grin.

"Did you enjoy that hit?"

"Swampert!" Wallace's expression hardened.

Swampert's fists glowed white—another Hammer Arm!

But before it could attack—

Slaking roared, twisting its monstrous strength to hurl Swampert into the ground.

BOOM!

Swampert groaned in pain, the battlefield cracked beneath it.

Slaking was too strong.

The crowd shivered—Slaking's ferocity was downright terrifying.

"Swampert! Use Earth Power!"

Wallace quickly called out.

Swampert gritted its teeth, summoning golden energy as the ground beneath them began to glow.

"Seed Bomb!"

Slaking ignored the attack, grabbing Swampert and hurling it again—left, right, then straight into the air!

"HULK SMASH!!!"

BOOM!

Earth Power detonated, sending tremors through the battlefield—Slaking roared in pain.

But it powered through.

A green glow gathered in its mouth—

And then—

A massive Seed Bomb blasted straight into Swampert's face.

Slaking immediately flopped onto the ground, taking a break.

"Swampert! Use Protect!"

Wallace panicked.

But it was too late.

The Seed Bomb exploded on impact—four times effective against Swampert!

When the dust settled—Swampert was knocked out cold.

"Swampert is unable to battle! Slaking wins!" Cynthia announced.

"…This…"

Wallace stared at Slaking, then at his fallen Swampert.

This Slaking was beyond ridiculous.

First, Smeargle had tormented him.

Now, Slaking was brutally overpowering him.

Dylan wasn't using tricks anymore—he was just crushing him outright.

---

---

---

# 252: Chapter 253: A Friend

Wallace recalled Swampert, hesitating over his next move. Milotic's physical defense wasn't great—if it took two hits from this absurdly strong Slaking, it probably wouldn't last.

No, that wouldn't work.

His gaze settled on the lazy, slouched Slaking, currently resting.

"Tentacruel!"

Wallace threw out his fifth Poké Ball, his final Pokémon for this Gym battle.

With a flash of white light, a blue jellyfish Pokémon appeared, its long tentacles supporting its weight.

A Water/Poison-type—Tentacruel.

As a Water-type specialist, Wallace's team performed best in aquatic arenas, and some of his strongest Pokémon needed water surfaces just to function properly.

For instance, Wailord and Luvdisc—without water, they could barely fight at all.

Compared to them, Tentacruel was at least usable on land, though far from ideal.

Honestly, reaching Champion-level using Pokémon like these was an achievement in itself.

Dylan watched, immediately guessing Wallace's plan.

"Tentacruel, use Toxic!"

Wallace wasted no time.

Tentacruel raised two tentacles, summoning a dark purple toxin before launching it at Slaking.

Still resting, Slaking didn't even try to dodge.

The poison seeped in instantly, dark energy pulsing across its fur. Purple blotches spread, and Slaking let out a pained roar.

It was badly poisoned!

"Not bad, Champion Wallace." Dylan gave an approving nod.

Yes, Slaking's Truant ability was a fatal weakness. Instead of brute force, Wallace had chosen the smarter route—status conditions like burn or poison.

Now, Slaking's clock was ticking.

"But how will you handle its next attack?"

Dylan's voice barely fell when Slaking suddenly stood, wild ferocity returning to its eyes.

"Charge in!"

Letting out a guttural snarl, Slaking lunged at Tentacruel with terrifying speed!

"Surf!" Wallace countered immediately.

With Slaking poisoned and held back by Truant, all he had to do was stall it out.

Sure, this wasn't the most "honorable" strategy, but as long as he could take down one of Dylan's Pokémon, it was worth it!

Tentacruel lifted its tentacles, gathering water energy.

Just as the tides began to surge—

"Sucker Punch!"

Slaking's eyes flashed with darkness as it vanished.

A shadow streaked behind Tentacruel.

"Tentacruel, watch out!" Wallace's eyes widened.

Too late.

A massive black-glowing fist swung down.

BOOM!

Tentacruel slammed into the floor, the rising water collapsing with it.

Even the battlefield trembled.

Though Sucker Punch wasn't super effective, Tentacruel's weak physical defense made the damage brutal—especially against Slaking's raw power.

"Protect!" Wallace reacted instantly.

Dylan, however, was quicker.

"Thunder Punch!"

Electricity crackled around Slaking's raised fist.

A violent wind surged, the punch barreling toward Tentacruel.

But just in time, a translucent shield formed around the jellyfish.

BOOM!

Thunder Punch crashed into the Protect barrier, failing to break through.

At that moment, purple energy pulsed from Slaking's body.

The second wave of Toxic damage.

As Slaking winced, its wild aura faded.

Truant kicked in again.

Yawning, Slaking flopped onto the ground once more.

"Ugh, what a waste," Lillie pouted. "If not for Truant, Slaking would've won already!"

"That's just how it is," Cynthia replied. "If Truant could be removed, Slaking wouldn't be such a rare pick."

After all, if Truant could be bred out, Slaking would be a top-tier powerhouse.

"Tentacruel, use Venoshock!"

Wallace seized the opening, giving his command.

Tentacruel raised its tentacles, unleashing a blast of toxic purple liquid straight at Slaking.

Slaking let out a pained snarl—

Because Venoshock's power doubled against poisoned targets, the attack landed hard.

Wallace's eyes sharpened.

"One more!"

This was it. He could take down Slaking!

Tentacruel fired another blast, but—

Slaking's aura changed.

Truant was over.

"Sucker Punch!"

Slaking vanished.

Tentacruel's attack missed entirely.

"Tentacruel, dodge—!" Wallace shouted, but it was useless.

A shadowy fist appeared behind Tentacruel, slamming into its head with crushing force.

BOOM!

The jellyfish's body dented inward before collapsing onto the ground.

"Protect!" Wallace called.

"Grab it!" Dylan countered.

Tentacruel struggled to lift its tentacles, attempting to shield itself—

But Slaking moved first.

Thick, powerful arms gripped several tentacles, squeezing tightly.

Then—

BOOM!

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

Slaking smashed Tentacruel against the ground, again and again.

The brutality of the scene left spectators in shock.

Just then, a deep purple glow flared—

The third wave of Toxic damage.

Slaking gritted its teeth, visibly wincing from the pain.

But it didn't let go.

With one final, devastating throw, Tentacruel was sent flying.

When the dust settled, Tentacruel lay motionless.

It had been beaten unconscious—just by being slammed around!

"Tentacruel is unable to battle! Slaking wins!"

Cynthia's voice rang through the battlefield.

The crowd fell into stunned silence.

Everyone stared at Slaking, breathing heavily like a war-hardened beast, their eyes filled with awe.

That was the third one.

Slaking had defeated three of Wallace's Champion-level Pokémon—back-to-back!

"This is insane! I think I'm in love with Gym Leader Dylan!"

"Oh my god, I want a Slaking now!"

"You say that, but trust me—if you actually train one, you'll lose your mind."

"Honestly, if not for Truant, I feel like this Slaking could sweep Wallace's entire team."

"When is Wallace stepping down? I think Dylan would make a great Champion for Hoenn."

The crowd buzzed with discussions. This wasn't just any battle—it was against the Champion of Hoenn, yet Wallace had already lost three Pokémon in a row.

"Milotic!"

Wallace's usually flawless face was now filled with tension as he sent out his ace once again.

He never imagined that battling a Normal-type team would push him this far. Sweat dripped down his forehead—at this point, he wasn't even thinking about winning. He just wanted to take down that Slaking and maybe defeat one more Pokémon if he was lucky.

Milotic landed gracefully, its elegant tail flicking through the air.

"Milotic, use Hydro Pump!" Wallace ordered immediately.

A brilliant column of water blasted forward, striking Slaking dead on. The sheer force sent water droplets spraying in every direction.

As the mist cleared, Slaking struggled to lift itself, but its body gave out, collapsing back onto the ground.

Fainted.

"Slaking is unable to battle! Milotic wins!" Cynthia announced.

Hah—!

In perfect sync, Wallace and half the spectators let out a collective sigh of relief.

Finally.

Wallace felt like a weight had been lifted—if he had let himself get swept by a single Slaking, who the hell would believe it?!

"Impressive, Champion Wallace. You actually managed to defeat my Slaking."

Dylan smiled as he recalled Slaking.

Wallace: "…"

Was that a compliment or an insult? He wasn't sure, but either way, he didn't want it.

He was the Champion of Hoenn, damn it!

"Well then, let's see how you handle this."

Dylan tossed out his next Poké Ball.

Wallace's expression turned serious. He had learned his lesson—none of Dylan's Pokémon were simple.

"Eevee, let's battle!"

With a flick of his wrist, Dylan released his third Pokémon.

"Bui!"

A small, fluffy Eevee landed gracefully in front of Dylan, its big round eyes brimming with determination.

"Eevee?"

Wallace frowned slightly, analyzing it.

It looked no different from a regular Eevee.

Except…

Dylan had put a baseball cap on it.

A cap-wearing Eevee?

"It doesn't seem all that special," Wallace muttered to himself.

He had seen plenty of Eevee before—while its evolved forms were powerful, Eevee itself was nothing remarkable.

It was cute, sure, but battles weren't won on appearance.

"Milotic, Hydro Pump!"

Wallace decided to test the waters.

Milotic's mouth glowed blue, charging up another massive jet of water before firing it straight at Eevee.

"Quick Attack."

Dylan's voice was calm.

Eevee's small body blurred, white streaks of light appearing behind it as it dodged the attack with ease, closing in on Milotic in an instant.

The Hydro Pump veered off-course, blasting past Eevee.

"Eevee, Bullet Seed!"

Eevee let out a sharp cry, green energy gathering around it. Suddenly, a vine sprouted from the ground, launching multiple glowing green seeds toward Milotic.

A Grass-type move?!

Wallace's eyes widened in shock.

"What the hell?!"

The seeds hit their mark, each one exploding on impact. Milotic cried out in pain, the Grass-type attack dealing heavy super-effective damage.

As the dust settled, thin green vines wrapped around Milotic's body, burrowing into its skin.

Parasitic roots.

"Leech Seed?!"

Wallace was stunned. That move wasn't part of Bullet Seed—how did this work?!

The vines began siphoning Milotic's energy, green light floating toward Eevee, restoring its health.

"Milotic, use Recover!"

Wallace forced himself to focus—Leech Seed was annoying, but it could be removed later. For now, he just had to restore Milotic's HP.

Milotic let out a soft, melodic cry, its body glowing with a healing light.

"Zippy Zap."

Wallace's brain short-circuited.

"WHAT."

Eevee leapt into the air, its body crackling with golden electricity before shooting a lightning bolt straight at Milotic.

BOOM!

The electric attack exploded on contact, forcing another agonized cry from Milotic.

Wallace felt his soul leave his body.

An Electric move too?!

Milotic wasn't heavily damaged, thanks to its high Special Defense, but—

As the sparks danced along its scales, its body suddenly froze.

Paralyzed.

Wallace's eye twitched violently.

Of course.

Of course it had a high paralysis rate.

He stood there, completely silent.

At first, he had assumed that Dylan's Smeargle was just a one-time gimmick.

Smeargle could copy any move, so it made sense that its moveset was ridiculous.

But this Eevee?

What the hell was up with this Eevee?!

Wallace had never seen these moves in his life.

Where the hell did Dylan get this thing?!

"Dylan."

Wallace took a deep breath, forcing himself to speak.

"Where. Did you get this Eevee?"

"Oh, this?"

Dylan smiled innocently.

"A friend gave it to me."

---

---

---

# 253: Chapter 254: Retire

"A friend gave it to me."

Hearing Dylan's response, Wallace pondered for a moment.

"Does your friend have any other Eevee?"

Wallace was quite interested in this uniquely special Eevee—he wanted one too.

"Professor Oak only had this one special Eevee. If Mr. Wallace is interested, I can help you ask."

Dylan was very polite.

"Professor Oak, huh? Then never mind." Wallace nodded. No wonder it was so special—it came from Professor Oak.

"Rotom, could you analyze it for me?"

Cynthia looked at the energetic Partner Eevee, curious about the moves it had just used.

"Of course, Roto~!" Rotom Pokédex liked polite people.

'Beep~ Partner Eevee, Normal-type, Evolution Pokémon

Ability: Adaptability

Moves: Veevee Volley, Bouncy Bubble, Buzzy Buzz, Sizzly Slide, Glitzy Glow, Baddy Bad, Sappy Seed, Freezy Frost, Sparkly Swirl…

Description: Due to its unstable genes, it has a wide variety of possible evolutions, but its population is extremely rare.'

Huh???

Even though Cynthia and Steven had prepared themselves mentally, they were still shocked by the analysis.

What were those moves?Did Eevee actually have so many hidden exclusive techniques?

"Dylan… this is incredible."

Steven was stunned. He had quickly counted—the Partner Eevee had a total of nine unique moves!Nine!!Have you ever seen a Pokémon with nine exclusive techniques?

"Normal, Water, Electric, Fire, Psychic, Dark, Grass, Ice, Fairy—it perfectly corresponds to Eevee's eight evolution types. This is astonishing."

Cynthia's beautiful eyes sparkled as she carefully examined the properties and effects of these nine moves in the Rotom Pokédex.

Except for the Normal-type Veevee Volley, the other eight moves were not only powerful but also had exceptional additional effects.

For example, the Buzzy Buzz that Partner Eevee just used had power comparable to Thunderbolt, but its paralysis chance was far higher than Thunderbolt.

Too strong!

"My brother once said that because Partner Eevee is a very special Eevee, it is born unable to evolve. But in return, its potential far surpasses other Eevee."

Lillie explained to the two of them. Yes, just like Partner Pikachu, Partner Eevee was naturally unable to evolve, but it had much greater potential and strength than an ordinary Pikachu or Eevee.

"These moves were also personally taught to Partner Eevee by my brother, one by one."

Well, actually, they were learned in the Growth Simulator.Dylan had discovered a mentor in Kanto who specialized in teaching Partner Moves from Let's Go Pikachu & Eevee.

"If I were to challenge Dylan, I would definitely not want to face his Normal-type team."

Steven stroked his chin, feeling certain about this. Even though Dylan had only revealed three of his main Pokémon, just that Smeargle alone was enough of a headache.

Challenging Dylan was a meaningful experience—after all, Dylan was undeniably powerful. Fighting against strong opponents was the best way to improve oneself.

He would definitely challenge Dylan in the future when he had the chance. But for now, he should thank Wallace—he had taken the hit first.

"Mr. Wallace has no chance of winning."

Cynthia nodded as she watched Partner Eevee and Milotic still engaged in battle.

Milotic was now paralyzed. Though the Marvel Scale ability had activated, raising its defense, it was meaningless.

Compared to the defensive boost, paralysis had too severe an impact on Milotic.Not only did it slow Milotic's movements, but the worst part was the occasional full-body paralysis.

Partner Eevee was already strong, and now that Milotic's threat level had plummeted due to paralysis, the battle was as good as over.

Sure enough, Milotic was soon defeated by Partner Eevee.

Then came the Ludicolo, still asleep. One Pompom Break from Partner Eevee, and Ludicolo was GG.

"Challenger Wallace has lost all five of his Pokémon. This challenge ends in failure."

Cynthia officially announced the battle's result. The onlookers gasped in shock.

The Hoenn Champion Wallace had just lost a Gym Challenge…

And in a 5v5 battle, he had only defeated one of the Gym Leader's Pokémon. If this news spread, all of Hoenn would be shaken!

Especially those who disliked Wallace—they would absolutely seize this opportunity to criticize him online.

"Dylan, you're amazing~ I'm not even close."

After the battle, Wallace recalled his fainted Ludicolo and looked at Dylan with a complicated expression.

He had hoped to use this Gym Challenge to show Dylan the power of his Water-type team, but instead, he was completely destroyed.

His Water-type team didn't even get a chance to shine, but Dylan had perfectly demonstrated the strength of the Normal-type Rota Gym.

"Mr. Wallace is very strong as well. I learned a lot."

Dylan remained humble.

Wallace's mouth twitched. Learned a lot from what?!

"We should leave now… my reputation is ruined~!"

Wallace glanced around and noticed the large crowd of spectators. He pressed his fingers against his forehead, feeling a headache coming on.

He didn't even need to check—by now, the internet was definitely flooded with headlines about how he got destroyed by a Kanto Gym Leader.

The group quickly left the battlefield and returned to the Hoenn League building, heading to Wallace's Champion's office.

Steven had also brought May along.

Dylan took a few extra glances at the cheerful and energetic girl.

She wore a tight-fitting tank top that accentuated her slender waist and graceful curves, paired with white shorts and an adorable hair accessory atop her head.

Without a doubt, this was May, the protagonist of the Pokémon Ruby & Sapphire series.In the anime, her name was May, while in the Pokémon Adventures manga, she was Sapphire.

Dylan could never forget watching Pokémon Evolutions: The Wish in his previous life—where Zinnia summoned Rayquaza, only for May to engage in a capture battle.

In the end, Mega Swampert landed an Ice Punch and one-shot Rayquaza, catching it effortlessly.Absolutely insane!

"This is May, daughter of Norman, the Gym Leader of Petalburg City."

Inside the office, Steven introduced May to the others. He had met her earlier when she delivered a package for Mr. Stone.

Seeing this talented and lively girl, Steven had taken a liking to her and kept an eye out for her growth.

"Hello, seniors~!"

May bowed politely, her voice crisp and sweet.

"May is a rookie Trainer who started her journey this year, but she's exceptionally talented. I think she'll be the champion of this year's Grand Festival."

Steven had high praise for May, seeing her as the future of Hoenn.

"Hehe~"

Hearing Steven's praise, May scratched the back of her head shyly but still puffed up her chest with pride.

"A promising talent."

Dylan agreed—May's potential was undeniable.

"I knew it. I'm trending."

Wallace checked his phone and, sure enough, saw himself on the hot searches.

Damn.

In just half an hour, several articles had dominated the Hoenn League's trending news.

In the comments, Wallace's haters and fans were fighting, while bystanders were watching the drama unfold.

There was no doubt about it—Wallace had gone viral.

"We should have picked a more private location for the battle," Dylan said, feeling a little awkward. Though, in all honesty, he had gone easy on Wallace.

"Maybe… but…" Wallace fell into deep thought.

Looking at the flood of headlines, he stole a glance at Steven. He suddenly felt like this was a golden opportunity.

Yes, later, he would go find President Goodshow and 'resign due to disgrace'. After all, as Hoenn's Champion, he had just been utterly defeated in public by a Gym Leader from Kanto.

That was basically an embarrassment to the entire Hoenn region!

How could he continue being Champion after such a disaster?

Perfect.

Wallace's mood instantly brightened. As for losing to Dylan so badly? He didn't even care.

Who was Dylan, after all?

That was the guy who could go head-to-head with Primal Groudon and Primal Kyogre. Losing to him was completely reasonable.

Surely, everyone would understand eventually.

"Oh, right, Mr. Wallace—please accept this," Dylan suddenly remembered something and handed Wallace an ornate box.

"What's this?" Wallace asked curiously, taking the box and opening it.

Inside was a green badge, set with a finely crafted gray-white gemstone representing the Normal-type symbol.

It was the Rota Badge.

"So pretty~!"

May peeked over, eyes sparkling as she caught sight of the badge.

"A Normal-type Rota Badge. You wouldn't refuse, would you, Mr. Wallace?"

Dylan smiled. Wallace may have lost pretty badly, but honestly, it wasn't his fault.

Aside from Slaking, both Smeargle and Partner Eevee were absolute nightmares for first-time opponents.

With so many unpredictable, overwhelmingly strong moves, even an experienced Trainer would have no idea how to counter them.

"Ohhh~ It really is the Rota Badge! So elegant! Dylan, I love it!"

Wallace had no intention of refusing. He held up the exquisite badge, eyes shining.

He had already done his research on Dylan and the Rota Gym—this badge was extremely valuable!

The real reason he had challenged Dylan? He wanted one of these badges!

Ahem… Though the outcome wasn't exactly what he had expected, he still got the badge in the end.

Besides, it wasn't like he begged Dylan for it—Dylan gave it to him willingly. So, no shame in accepting it, right?

Not to mention, after this battle, his reputation was in tatters. Getting this badge at least helped heal his wounded pride a little.

"Tsk~ It suits me perfectly! What do you all think? Doesn't it look amazing?"

Wallace pinned the Rota Badge to the left side of his chest, the shimmering gemstone gleaming brightly.

"This is a Rota Badge that I worked very hard to earn!"

He turned to Steven and the others with a proud expression.

???

Worked hard? You got completely destroyed!

Steven nearly facepalmed, his mind screaming in protest.

"The badge is beautiful. No wonder it's worth over 10 million Pokédollars each."

Despite his inner complaints, Steven still complimented the badge's craftsmanship. As the heir to Devon Corporation, he wasn't exactly lacking in money—10 million wasn't a huge deal to him.

But the significance of the Rota Badge was different.

This was a badge personally awarded by Dylan!

"Wait—10 million?!" May was shocked.

Cynthia also looked surprised. She knew that Dylan's Rota Badges were extremely rare and that he had only given out a few.

But this expensive?!

Cynthia came from a well-off family—her grandmother was the head of the Celestic Town Historical Research Institute and a Pokémon Professor.

She had never struggled financially, but spending 10 million Pokédollars on a single badge?

That was just… ridiculous.

"That's right. Each of Dylan's Rota Badges costs over 10 million. And apparently, the most exclusive Legendary Rota Badge is worth 20–30 million!" Wallace grinned.

"Hissss~" May sucked in a breath. Holy crap.

"Come to think of it, Dylan—what's your family background?" Steven asked, curious. Dylan was way too rich—not to mention, shockingly generous.

"The Silph Co.'s largest shareholder is my father," Dylan answered with a humble smile.

Ah. That explains it.

Steven and the others instantly understood.

Wallace, meanwhile, sighed. Another filthy-rich second-generation heir.

"Dylan, can I challenge you too?!" May suddenly asked, voice bright and eager.

She had already collected all eight Hoenn Gym Badges and was preparing to enter the Grand Festival.

That's why she was currently in Slateport—she had been training in the area.

"Of course you can." Dylan smiled.

Just then, the office door swung open.

"Wallace!"

President Goodshow stormed in, his eyes locked onto Wallace.

"President?"

"Wallace, the public is in an uproar!"

Goodshow looked exasperated. He had just received reports of Wallace's battle with Dylan—he understood that Wallace wasn't strong enough to beat Dylan.

After all, he was well aware of Dylan's ridiculous strength.

But the problem was, Wallace had been utterly crushed in public.

As Hoenn's Champion, he had been annihilated by a Normal-type team.

Now, the situation was spiraling out of control.

Wallace's position as Champion had always been controversial. He had never officially defeated Steven to claim the title—many people in Hoenn had never truly accepted him.

And now, this scandal had exploded.

The Hoenn League Forums were in chaos.

"No need to panic, President."

Wallace elegantly lifted his tea cup, taking a slow sip. His voice was calm, steady, and graceful.

"You have a solution?" Goodshow and the others leaned in, intrigued.

Right now, Wallace's reputation was tanking hard.

As Hoenn's Champion, he was supposed to represent the League's strength. His humiliating defeat had put the entire region in a bad light.

"It's quite simple."

Wallace's handsome, porcelain-like face curled into a charming smile.

Then, his eyes landed on his dear friend.

Steven's heart sank—a bad feeling creeping up his spine.

This bastard is going to throw me under the bus.

"Just have Steven return as Champion," Wallace said, beaming.

Steven's vision went black.

He had just suffered through the grief of his father's passing, and now Wallace—this heartless bastard—wanted to rob him of his freedom!

Son of a—!

Goodshow's eyes lit up. This was a great idea!!

And then…

"Dylan! You're a legend! You just forced Hoenn's Champion into retirement!!"

(End of Chapter)

# 254: Chapter 255: I Want Them All

"Dylan, you're insane! Did you really just make the Hoenn Champion retire?!"

Back at the hotel, Leaf gave Dylan a huge thumbs-up.

"???"

Dylan's face darkened. What the hell do you mean I made him retire?

"Get out of here with that nonsense. Wallace is probably laughing in his sleep tonight."

Dylan rolled his eyes.

Sure enough, the Hoenn League had issued an official announcement that afternoon:

"This afternoon, Mr. Wallace, upon deep self-reflection, has decided he needs further training to improve himself. Therefore, he has chosen to pass the title of Hoenn Champion back to Mr. Steven Stone. We sincerely thank Mr. Wallace for his three years of dedicated service to the Hoenn League. The official Champion Succession Ceremony will be held tomorrow."

You had to admit, the Hoenn League moved insanely fast.

Not that they were throwing Wallace under the bus or anything. As far as the League was concerned, Wallace had been a responsible and dedicated Champion, and they had been quite satisfied with him.

Honestly, both Wallace and Steven had been excellent Champions, even if their personal interests lay elsewhere—Wallace with Contests and Steven with digging up rare stones.

But the public backlash from Wallace's battle with Dylan was just too much.

Every region's citizens take great pride in their League and their Champion.

And Wallace's brutal defeat wasn't just about losing—people could have understood if he had simply lost. After all, he was a Water-type specialist, and the battle wasn't fought on a Water-friendly field.

But the real problem was how badly he lost.

A 5v5 battle, and he only managed to defeat one of Dylan's Pokémon. The other two were still in excellent condition.

That…

That was just embarrassing.

Still, when Dylan recalled Wallace's beaming, overjoyed face after the match, he could tell there wasn't a hint of regret in his "retirement." If anything, Wallace probably wanted to throw a party to celebrate his newfound freedom.

Steven, on the other hand…

That forced, bitter smile he wore at the time? Absolutely priceless.

Honestly, these two were such a pair of best friends.

"Mm?" Leaf tilted her head in confusion.

Eh, whatever. Not her problem.

"Come here, Lillie! I bought a ton of clothes for you today. Let's take a look!"

"Huh? Oh! Sure!"

Leaf pulled Lillie over excitedly. She had gone on a shopping spree today—with Dylan footing the bill, of course!

Dylan had just sat down on the sofa when his phone suddenly blew up with notifications.

—

Professor Oak: My boy is unbeatable! 😎

Chairman Goodshow: Hahaha~ Incredible! You made Wallace retire?! Dylan, my top general, is truly unstoppable! 😂

Lance: Not bad, my junior. Are the Champions from other regions really this weak?

Alder: Pffft—I'm dying! Dylan, you actually forced Hoenn's Champion to step down?! HAHAHA! 🤣

Alder: By the way, when are you coming to Unova again? I was talking to Caitlin recently—maybe I should introduce you two? She's a super gorgeous young lady from a very wealthy family! A perfect, high-class beauty! (👀)

Blue: HOLY SHIT, DYLAN!! 😲🔥

—

Dylan scrolled through the messages, speechless.

How do these guys get news so fast?!

Then again, it wasn't that surprising. Hoenn had already been in the spotlight due to the Groudon vs. Kyogre incident, and now, out of nowhere, they were changing Champions.

That was huge news.

Dylan replied to each of them, especially Alder—this guy was always trying to set him up with someone.

Still, the whole situation had worked in Dylan's favor.

Wallace went viral.

Dylan went viral.

And Rota Gym became a hot topic worldwide.

People from other regions were now incredibly curious about Rota Gym. After all, Wallace had been Hoenn's Champion—getting nearly swept by someone was a big deal.

Just as Dylan finished replying to the messages, another chat exploded.

—

Officer Jenny (Mio): @Everyone—DID YOU SEE THAT?! This is what a real Gym Leader should be like! Who said Gym Leaders are weaker than Champions?! Dylan just crushed the Hoenn Champion!

She also attached a battle video of Dylan vs. Wallace.

Officer Jenny (Mio): Study this! Learn from it! If you all were even half as good as Dylan, I wouldn't have to babysit you all the time! (😤)

Cerulean Gym—Viole: I can't learn that… (🥺)

Cinnabar Gym—Blaine: Holy crap, this kid is insane!

Cerulean Gym—Lily: (Sorry, I'm useless.) 😭

Pewter Gym—Flint: How the hell am I supposed to learn from this?!

Celadon Gym—Erika: Dylan will always be my idol!! (😍🔥)

Viridian Gym—Giovanni: Heh. Impressive as always. @Dylan, when are you coming back to Kanto? We should have a proper meeting.

Saffron Gym—Sabrina: (Confused emoji.)

—

Dylan: I'll be back in Kanto in the next couple of days. I'll make sure to visit, Mr. Giovanni.

After replying, Dylan leaned back in thought.

This was the second time Giovanni had reached out to him.

Interesting.

Once he got back to Kanto, he would definitely pay him a visit.

—

The Next Day – Slateport Airport

"If you ever want to visit Hoenn again, you're always welcome," Steven said with a polite smile.

Even though, deep inside, he was screaming in despair—he really didn't want to be Champion again.

But, for now, he had to smile and wave.

"I definitely will when I get the chance." Dylan returned the smile.

"I feel the same way. This trip to Hoenn has been very rewarding," Cynthia added. "If you ever come to Sinnoh, let me know. I'll personally show you around."

"If Mr. Dylan ever visits Sinnoh, I'd love to challenge your Gym as well," Cynthia said, her golden eyes flashing with excitement.

She was genuinely interested in facing the Rota Gym.

"Of course. I'd love to battle you too, Miss Cynthia," Dylan smirked.

"Ohhh~! If that happens, you have to let me know!" Wallace's eyes sparkled. A battle between Dylan and Cynthia? He had to watch that live!

As their plane took off, Wallace and Steven watched from the ground, reflecting on everything that had happened.

They both knew—this event, this meeting with Dylan and Cynthia—was part of that same "fate."

But then…

"AIZEN!!!"

Wallace gritted his teeth in fury.

On his phone screen was a photoshopped image of him and Steven—wearing wigs and dresses in a "romantic" pose.

There was no doubt—this was Aizen's doing!

"DAMN YOU, AIZEN!!!"

And in the comment section, the likes and shares were skyrocketing.

Just then, his phone rang.

It was Lisia.

"Wallace… men and men… it won't work out."

"???"

Wallace was left staring at his phone, completely speechless.

…

Kanto Region

Dylan and his companions finally returned to Rota Street.

"Whew~ Finally back home!"

At the entrance to Rota Street, Leaf stretched her arms wide, sighing in comfort.

Even though their trip to Hoenn had lasted less than a week, it felt like they had experienced so much!

Especially for her—being Dylan's "double" was exhausting!

"Yeah, it really feels like we were gone for a long time," Lillie nodded in agreement.

"Let's go. Everything's already set up."

Dylan motioned for them to follow—he had already made the necessary arrangements in advance.

The three of them made their way to a dye workshop in Rota Street—one that Dylan had specially contacted beforehand. In fact, he had gone a step further and bought out the entire place.

"Mr. Dylan!"

"Jack, how's the preparation going?"

Dylan stepped inside, where a middle-aged man greeted him. This was Jack, the workshop's former owner.

His family had been in the dyeing business for generations, making them masters of their craft.

"Of course, Mr. Dylan! Based on the reference images you provided, we have painstakingly recreated every detail—there won't be even a fraction of a mistake!"

"But… is this really happening?"

Jack's eyes gleamed with anticipation.

"Of course. If you all do a perfect job, everyone will be rewarded. But I won't tolerate any leaks about this project. Understand?"

Dylan smiled, his tone light but firm.

"Please don't worry, Mr. Dylan!" Jack responded immediately, his posture straightening.

"Good. Let's do it here."

Dylan followed Jack to a spacious hall. After scanning the area to ensure it was big enough, he released a Poké Ball.

With a flash of white light, a deep, rumbling dragon's roar echoed through the workshop.

Rayquaza rose majestically, its piercing golden eyes gazing down at the scene.

"OH MY GOD! It's Rayquaza!!"

Jack almost fainted from excitement.

Dylan wasn't lying—he was really about to dye Rayquaza!

Jack could hardly believe it. He was about to work on a Legendary Pokémon.

Meanwhile, Rayquaza remained proud and imposing, its regal aura undiminished. But its sharp eyes soon turned to Dylan, filled with curiosity.

"Jack, bring out the sketches."

"Right away!" Jack rushed off and quickly returned with a thick sketchbook.

Leaf and Lillie leaned in, curious about what they were about to see.

"Mr. Dylan, this is the black Rayquaza you requested."

Jack flipped to the first page, revealing a stunningly detailed drawing of a sleek, jet-black Rayquaza.

"If you want this exact color, the dyeing process will take around two to three days. As per your requirements, the dye will be waterproof, durable, and completely safe for the skin. However, the color will need to be reapplied every three months to maintain its vibrancy."

"And that's assuming there's no intense battling—if the battles are too extreme, there's a chance the dye might wear off."

Jack explained this seriously, though he secretly found it a bit unnecessary.

Who in the world was capable of beating Rayquaza so badly that it lost its paint job?

That was impossible, right?

Rayquaza lowered its head slightly, staring at the drawing.

Then its golden eyes lit up.

MOTHER'S BLACK SKIN!!

"Dylan!! I WANT THIS! I NEED THIS!!"

Rayquaza's mental voice rang out in Dylan's head, urging him excitedly.

It wanted to be BLACK too! It wanted to be a Shiny Rayquaza!

"Calm down," Dylan chuckled, patting Rayquaza's side.

"What other options do you have?" Dylan asked Jack.

"Aside from the black version you requested, we also designed several alternate colors. For example, take a look at this one."

Jack flipped to the next page, revealing an intimidating, blood-red Rayquaza, with jet-black wings.

Rayquaza's eyes gleamed like lanterns.

THIS RED LOOKS AMAZING TOO!

"This one features a dark crimson body, exuding an aura of power and mystery," Jack described.

"Dylan! I want this one too!"

Rayquaza's voice roared in Dylan's mind.

"And here's another—gold represents the highest level of prestige. This version radiates absolute dominance!"

Jack flipped another page, revealing a pure-gold Rayquaza.

"My Rayquaza clan was born to be dominant!"

Rayquaza was completely immersed in the possibilities.

"Ivory white… Platinum gray… Fairy pink…"

Rayquaza was losing itself in a sea of colors.

"Dylan, which one should I pick?!"

Rayquaza's voice was filled with conflict.

But it didn't take long before it made its decision.

"I WANT THEM ALL!!"

The Rayquaza clan had no limits—it would claim EVERY color!

(End of Chapter)

# 255: Chapter 256: I’m Here to Challenge You!

"What do you mean, 'I want them all'?!

Dylan's eye twitched. The hell do you mean, all of them? You trying to get a rainbow-colored black or something?

"Let's start with black first. We can switch colors later," Dylan decided after a moment.

Riding a black Rayquaza was something he had always wanted to do. Even if he couldn't ride the real thing yet, at least he could get a painted version to enjoy for now.

Besides, in Dylan's mind, he was bound to capture a Rayquaza sooner or later anyway.

"Got it!" Jack nodded.

"Rayquaza, stay here and work with them."

Rayquaza was huge, and giving it a full-body color treatment was no small project. Even at full speed, Jack's team would need two to three days to complete the process.

Dylan had demanded perfection, after all.

"Go ahead, Heir of the Sky!" Rayquaza was beyond excited to begin.

Leaving the dye workshop behind, Dylan returned to Rota Gym.

"Ahhh, I'm so exhausted. I need to rest for a bit."

Leaf stretched her shoulders as she headed toward her room. She had already picked a room in Rota Gym, since she was practically an employee at this point.

"Oh, right, Dylan. If someone comes looking for me in the next few days, can you take care of them?" She suddenly turned back.

"A friend of yours?" Dylan asked, raising an eyebrow.

"A little brother, actually. He escaped from the same organization as me. I finally got back in touch with him yesterday and told him to come to Rota Gym."

Leaf yawned cutely.

"His name is Silver. He looks scary and cold, but he's a good kid."

She suddenly became serious.

"He's a year younger than me. Dylan, if possible, I'd like you to take him as your student."

"My apprentices don't have it easy," Dylan joked.

Not just anyone could become his apprentice. But truthfully, he was only teasing Leaf.

Silver's potential was undeniable.

The Pokémon Adventures protagonists—Red, Green, Blue, Yellow, Gold, Silver, and Crystal—were all legendary figures in their own right.

"Hehe~ I know! But Silver is really talented—way more than me."

Leaf's bright blue eyes sparkled with confidence in her younger brother.

"Got it. I'll see what he's capable of," Dylan nodded. An unexpected bonus, huh?

Leaf and Lillie both went to rest. After traveling by plane for so long, it was only natural to feel exhausted.

Dylan, however, headed to the backyard.

As soon as he stepped outside, he spotted Yellow playing with a Pikachu.

"Oh? You're back, Gym Leader Dylan!"

Yellow's golden eyes lit up as she ran over excitedly, hugging Pikachu in her arms.

"Yellow, how've you been?"

Dylan ruffled the little girl's soft cheeks with a smile.

Her eyes were so pure and innocent—looking at her made him feel strangely at peace.

Dylan loved kids like Yellow.

Yeah… Yellow was his true successor.

Selene? She had gone rogue.

Dylan was 100% certain that Selene calling everyone "Boss" or "Old Man" had absolutely nothing to do with him.

He was a bright, sunny Gym Leader!

Certified by the League and his fans!

It was even written in bold letters on the official site!

"Mm~ I'm doing great! Selene took me out for a trip! Oh, and look, Gym Leader Dylan—this Pikachu followed me from Viridian Forest!"

Yellow's smile was as pure as a sunbeam as she proudly held up the electric mouse.

"Pika?" Pikachu blinked its big eyes, staring at Dylan with curiosity.

"Very cute. So, does this little one want to stay with you?"

Dylan gently rubbed Pikachu's chin.

Yellow had even attached a small flower to Pikachu's left ear, making it look even cuter.

"I think so! It really likes me, and I really like it too!"

Yellow looked down at Pikachu with a shy smile, her eyes filled with warmth.

"Pika~ Chu!" Pikachu rubbed its cheek against Yellow's, showing its affection.

"Good. That's your bond forming."

Dylan smiled.

With her Viridian powers, Yellow naturally attracted Pokémon. This Pikachu choosing to follow her was a perfect example of that connection.

"As your teacher, I still haven't officially given you a Pokémon… Speaking of which, you seem to get along well with that Screamtail, don't you?"

Dylan pondered.

When he took on Selene as an apprentice, he had given her a Litten.

He couldn't treat Yellow unfairly.

Yellow often helped take care of the Pokémon in the backyard. Dylan actually had a lot of Pokémon—some that he wasn't actively training.

Lillie and Yellow would often assist in looking after them.

If he remembered correctly, Yellow had a particularly strong bond with a Screamtail.

That Screamtail had great potential, and since it was still young, its strength wasn't too overwhelming.

More importantly, Yellow's pure heart made her the perfect match for it.

"Eh?" Yellow blinked in surprise.

"I'm entrusting that Pokémon to you. Make sure to take good care of it, alright?" Dylan smiled warmly.

"But, Gym Leader Dylan… I'm not even a Trainer yet…"

Yellow looked happy, but also a bit hesitant.

"Doesn't matter. Look at Selene—she wasn't a Trainer when I gave her Litten, but that didn't stop her from running around challenging people every day."

Dylan waved it off.

Sure, the official League rules said you had to be 16 years old to get a Trainer license and start a journey.

But in reality, plenty of kids owned Pokémon before that.

This was called getting a head start.

Look at Paldea—their Elite Four had a 9-year-old.

In Pokémon Scarlet & Violet: The Indigo Disk, it was revealed that Poppy was already 9 years old.

Despite being an Elite Four member, she still wanted to enroll in Blueberry Academy the following year.

That was wild.

A 9-year-old Elite Four member!

Poppy was the definition of "age doesn't matter."

Some people spend a lifetime training Pokémon and never reach Elite status.

Meanwhile, some kids hadn't even started their journey and were already destroying the competition.

Dylan hoped his apprentices would get that same head start.

After all, his goal was for Yellow to become one of Kanto's Elite Four.

If she could start early, that would be ideal.

"Thank you, Gym Leader Dylan~!"

Yellow's bright, innocent smile was heartwarming.

"Do your best, Yellow. I know you won't lose to Blue."

Dylan ruffled her golden hair with a grin.

Leaving Rota Gym, Yellow was bound to draw attention.

With Rota Gym's fame skyrocketing, and Selene being the first apprentice to leave, low-profile wasn't an option.

By the time Yellow set off on her journey, people would definitely compare her to Selene.

But Dylan had a feeling…

Yellow wouldn't care one bit.

"Mm-mm!! I won't embarrass you, Gym Leader Dylan!"

Yellow tightened her grip on the Pikachu in her arms, her small face filled with determination.

"Good! That's the spirit! I believe in you."

Dylan patted Yellow's shoulder, pleased with his student's resolve.

Afterward, he released all his Pokémon and walked over to the small pond in the backyard.

"Keep growing strong, okay?"

Dylan spoke gently, looking down at the fish Pokémon floating lifelessly in the water.

The fish tilted its head slightly, staring blankly at Dylan, as if it wasn't sure what was happening.

Its fins were gray, and its body was a dull lavender color. Its eyes were completely lifeless, and it had a pair of pink lips—yeah, this thing was ugly as hell.

It was a Feebas—one of Wallace's Milotic's offspring.

But what Dylan hadn't expected was that this Feebas was shiny!

That's right—this was a shiny Feebas. And yet, Wallace had handed it over to Dylan without hesitation.

Man… Wallace was actually a great guy.

As thanks, Dylan had gifted Wallace a Iron Bundle—a Paradox Pokémon from the future that was both powerful and extremely rare.

A fair trade for Wallace's trust.

Beep—Scanning…

Feebas, Water Type, Fish PokémonAbility: Swift SwimMoves: Splash, Tackle, Haze (Egg Move)

Description: Due to its naturally ragged fins and unappealing appearance, no one ever pays attention to it. It can survive in both the ocean and freshwater lakes.

Once this shiny Feebas evolved into a shiny Milotic, though… it would be absolutely gorgeous.

Dylan's water-type team was already forming in his mind.

"Rain teams are a must… and then…"

If he was going to build a water team, then having rain support was essential.

There were only three Pokémon with the Drizzle ability: Politoed, Pelipper, and Kyogre.

Politoed and Pelipper were both solid choices, so he'd definitely need them.

"Swampert is a must, and I'll also need a Palafin."

Swampert was one of Dylan's favorite Pokémon, so of course, he had to train one. It would also serve as his team's Mega Evolution user.

Dylan already had a few strong water-types on hand—Toxapex, Iron Bundle, and Wave Crash.

But those were already too powerful to use against rookie challengers.

"Too many good choices…"

Dylan sighed. There were so many amazing water-types, making it hard to decide.

But he wasn't in a rush.

Meanwhile, news spread quickly—Rota Gym had officially reopened, and within a day, the announcement had spread all across Kanto.

The moment Dylan resumed accepting challenges, a flood of trainers arrived at his doorstep.

By the second day, the number of challengers was insane—with 30% of them coming from outside Kanto.

And then, three days later…

Dylan finally got his "Shiny" Rayquaza.

He had to admit—Jack and his team had done a flawless job. This artificial shiny Rayquaza was so perfectly painted that it was indistinguishable from the real thing.

Even its scales reflected light just like an actual shiny Rayquaza!

Rayquaza was thrilled with its new look. It couldn't stop admiring itself in the clear pond—turning left and right, checking every angle.

Five days passed in the blink of an eye.

During this time, Leaf had already left, and her little brother, Silver, had never shown up.

According to Leaf, Silver was "shy" and probably just "too nervous" to visit.

And now…

It was time for Dylan's day off.

Dylan and Lillie left Rota Street and headed for Viridian City.

Viridian wasn't far from Rota, and with Rayquaza as a ride, they got there in no time.

But one thing was clear.

Yeah, White Dragon was still the more comfortable ride.

"Viridian Gym… Brother, are you going to meet Giovanni?"

Lillie asked curiously as she looked at the majestic Viridian Gym in front of them.

"Yeah. We already made an appointment."

Dylan's golden eyes flickered as he gazed at Viridian Gym.

Then, he took a step forward, leading Lillie toward the entrance.

Giovanni! I'm here to challenge you!

# 256: Chapter 257: I Am the Strongest Man in Kanto!

Viridian Gym—also known as Evergreen Gym.

For a long time, it was considered the strongest Gym in Kanto, led by Giovanni, known as "Giovanni of the Earth."

Giovanni held great prestige in Kanto and was regarded as one of the most outstanding Gym Leaders of his time.

Dylan and Lillie stepped into Viridian Gym, finding themselves in a vast, grandly designed hall.

Straight ahead was a battlefield, built in a manner that perfectly suited Giovanni's expertise in Ground-type Pokémon.

"Gym Leader Dylan, welcome. I am Matori, Secretary to Giovanni. He has been expecting you."

A cool and elegant woman in business attire approached them, speaking in a calm and professional tone.

Dylan glanced at Matori—he recognized her as a member of Team Rocket.

"Please, follow me."

Matori gave a slight bow, gesturing for them to proceed.

The three of them walked into Giovanni's private office inside Viridian Gym.

There, seated on a leather sofa, was Giovanni, brewing tea in a composed manner.

"Dylan, it's been a while."

Giovanni stood up, a subtle smile appearing on his sharp, slightly gaunt face as he extended his hand.

"It has been a while indeed, Giovanni. Quite the refined taste you have."

Dylan shook Giovanni's hand, glancing at the tea kettle steaming gently on the table.

This wasn't their first meeting—they had met once before at Silph Co.

"Tea is not only a simple pleasure, but also a way to calm the mind and reflect on one's past actions."

Giovanni gave a faint chuckle before gesturing toward the seats.

"Please, have a seat."

"Thank you."

Dylan and Lillie took their seats across from Giovanni.

"Recently, you have become quite the center of attention in Kanto—capturing multiple high-ranking Team Rocket members and making Rota Gym more renowned than ever."

Matori poured tea for the three of them as Giovanni spoke in his deep, charismatic voice.

"A young hero indeed. At your age, I certainly wasn't as accomplished."

Giovanni's deep eyes studied Dylan closely.

If not for Dylan, Team Rocket wouldn't be in its current state.

Many of its Kanto executives had been captured by Dylan. While people like Proton, Petrel, and Archer weren't the strongest, their absence had severely disrupted Team Rocket's operations.

"You overestimate me, Giovanni. I was just lucky." Dylan remained modest.

"You're too humble. Luck is nothing more than an excuse for those without talent." Giovanni took a sip of tea, smiling faintly.

"Whether it's an individual or an event, success is never dictated by luck alone—that is merely an excuse used by the incompetent."

"Dylan, you are exceptional, and that excellence is undeniable."

Giovanni's evaluation of Dylan was incredibly high.

He was one of the few people who truly understood Dylan's strength.

And that's exactly why he sighed inwardly—as long as Dylan was around, Team Rocket's dreams of domination would be nearly impossible.

"No wonder people speak so highly of him."

Lillie had remained quietly listening, but she absorbed every word.

She had to admit—from the moment they arrived, Giovanni's speech, demeanor, and presence had been truly captivating.

It was no surprise that Viridian Gym had maintained such an outstanding reputation, even being ranked as the strongest Gym in Kanto.

Giovanni must be an immensely skilled Trainer.

"Dylan, I have met many brilliant young Trainers, even ones who left me in awe."

Giovanni's piercing gaze locked onto Dylan.

He had encountered all sorts of prodigies—people like Red and Blue, who he genuinely respected as monsters of talent.

But even they were overshadowed by Dylan.

"Yet, next to you, they pale in comparison. This era belongs to you, Dylan."

Giovanni concluded his thoughts with absolute certainty.

"You flatter me, Giovanni. I wouldn't dare make such a claim." Dylan remained humble.

"Haha…"

Giovanni let out a low chuckle.

"Dylan, I invited you here today to discuss something important. But before that… I'd like to battle you."

His deep, magnetic voice carried a serious intensity.

"I want to battle you at full power—as a Trainer, nothing held back. What do you say?"

A full-power battle?

Dylan raised an eyebrow. Was Giovanni being serious, or just testing the waters?

"Of course. It would be an honor to battle you, Giovanni." Dylan responded with a calm smile.

Even if Giovanni wasn't truly going all out, battling him was still an invaluable opportunity.

The two walked over to Viridian Gym's battlefield, standing across from each other.

As the scoreboard lit up, their half-body portraits appeared on either side of the screen.

"The match will now begin. The competitors are Trainer Giovanni and Trainer Dylan."

"The battle format is 6v6, with both sides allowed to switch Pokémon freely. The match ends when one side's entire team is defeated."

"Begin!"

Matori, acting as the referee, announced the start of the battle.

"A full battle, huh? It's been a while since I've had one of these."

Dylan chuckled softly.

This would be his first time engaging in a formal 6v6 battle.

Most of his previous battles had been Gym Matches, which came with rules and restrictions.

A full battle meant he could switch freely and use any Pokémon he wanted.

Giovanni stood tall and imposing, one hand in his pocket while casually tossing a Poké Ball in his other hand.

His black, high-end suit emphasized his broad, muscular frame, and his short, dark hair framed a face that remained calm and unreadable.

But something was shifting.

The atmosphere was changing.

The scoreboard's randomizer spun rapidly before finally stopping on Giovanni's side—meaning he would send out his Pokémon first.

"Dylan."

Giovanni looked up, his expression slowly twisting into a menacing smirk.

His deep voice dropped into a dangerous tone, his entire aura transforming.

Suddenly, the room felt heavy.

A dark, sinister pressure began to radiate from him.

Seated in the viewing area, Lillie's eyes widened in shock.

The difference was staggering.

In the office, Giovanni had been calm, charismatic, and dignified.

But now—he looked nothing like a respectable Gym Leader.

"This isn't right… why does he seem so different when he battles?"

Lillie shivered.

Even more bizarre…

Giovanni's earlier words had mocked Lance.

His tone and expression were filled with contempt.

He genuinely despised Lance.

But… why?

As far as Lillie knew, there was no major conflict between Giovanni and Lance.

So why was Giovanni acting like he had a deep grudge against him?

Lillie felt uneasy.

"Go, Golem!"

Giovanni threw his Poké Ball, summoning a massive, powerhouse Golem that crashed onto the battlefield.

Its rock-hard body resembled a giant boulder, with limbs and a head protruding from its core.

Beep—Scanning…

Golem, Rock/Ground Type, Megaton Pokémon

Ability: Rock Head

Moves: Stone Edge, Double-Edge, Explosion, Earthquake, Stealth Rock, Heavy Slam…

Description: Its body is as tough as stone—strong enough to withstand explosions. It sheds its rocky skin once a year.

"This Golem is insanely strong!"

This Golem was definitely top-tier!

Listening to the Rotom Pokédex's analysis, Lillie was more and more certain—this was one of Giovanni's main Pokémon.

But what really unsettled her was Giovanni himself.

His posture had changed, his face darkened, and his cold, piercing gaze was completely different from the calm, composed leader he had been in the office.

What's going on?

Did Giovanni always change this much when he battled?

And why had he made those comments about Lance?

Lillie had never heard of any major conflict between Giovanni and Lance, but judging by his tone, Giovanni clearly had some deep-seated hostility toward him.

Lillie frowned.

Something wasn't right.

Dylan's golden eyes flickered as he studied Golem.

A true main Pokémon, huh?

That meant Giovanni wasn't holding back at all.

"Well, Golem's weaknesses are pretty well-known," Dylan muttered, smirking slightly as he tossed his Poké Ball.

"Go—Toxapex!"

A small, deep-blue creature with spiny, violet tentacles emerged onto the battlefield.

It lifted its two front limbs, revealing the sinister face hidden beneath its shell.

"Toxapex, huh?"

Giovanni's dark smile didn't waver.

"This is my domain, Dylan. You move first."

His hands stayed tucked into his pockets, but his expression was eerily calm—almost too calm.

"Then I won't hold back—Toxapex, Toxic Spikes!"

Dylan's voice rang clear as his Toxapex swiftly unleashed a wave of toxic energy.

Dark purple spikes scattered across Giovanni's side of the field, embedding into the ground.

Giovanni's smirk widened slightly.

"Stealth Rock," he ordered.

Dylan's eyes narrowed.

Golem lifted its arms, summoning jagged, floating rocks that hovered ominously around Dylan's side of the field.

At the same time, Toxapex's Toxic Spikes exploded into the air, dispersing a sinister purple glow before settling back into the battlefield.

Both sides had successfully set up hazards.

"Your strategies are already widely known, Dylan," Giovanni remarked casually.

"Kanto's Trainers have improved dramatically thanks to you—this is your doing."

His voice wasn't mocking—it was simply stating a fact.

Dylan remained silent, letting him continue.

"As more and more Trainers challenged Rota Gym, your strategies became widely adopted. Many of those who suffered ended up learning from you."

The Toxic Spikes strategy was infamous at this point.

Poison-type Pokémon were rising in usage across Kanto, all because of Dylan's influence.

So Giovanni countered with Stealth Rock?

Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"Toxapex, use Scald!"

Toxapex extended its tentacles, shooting a boiling stream of water straight at Golem.

Water-type—quadruple effective against Rock/Ground Pokémon!

Even with Toxapex's mediocre Special Attack, this would be devastating.

"Rollout."

Giovanni's low, confident voice came right on cue.

Golem suddenly jerked sideways, rolling into a high-speed somersault, dodging the water blast at the last second.

In midair, its head and limbs retracted, turning it into a giant boulder as it slammed into the ground, landing smoothly.

Dylan narrowed his eyes.

As soon as Golem landed, the Toxic Spikes activated, releasing a burst of poisonous energy.

For a brief moment, Golem stiffened—a sign that it had been poisoned.

But then it kept moving.

"Excellent endurance," Dylan thought.

When poisoned, Pokémon typically flinched for a second, but Golem recovered almost instantly.

That meant Giovanni had specifically trained it to resist the initial poison delay.

And now, Golem was spinning toward Toxapex at full force.

10 meters…

5 meters…

3 meters…

1 meter…!

"Baneful Bunker!"

Dylan's voice rang out.

"Earthquake," Giovanni countered.

Toxapex's defensive barrier expanded, glowing dark purple, shielding itself completely.

But right before Golem made contact, it suddenly flipped backward, its massive body crashing into the ground.

And then a shockwave erupted across the battlefield.

Earthquake!

The powerful tremors shook the entire Gym, sending waves of force toward Toxapex's barrier.

But Baneful Bunker held firm, negating the damage.

Dylan's eyes narrowed slightly.

This wasn't just random movement—Giovanni had baited him.

He made it look like Golem would directly attack, but instead, it had feigned an approach and immediately counterattacked.

Dylan exhaled softly.

"Not bad."

He was right—this wasn't the first time Giovanni had pulled off this trick.

There was no way Golem could flip backward so precisely in such a short window unless it had practiced countless times.

If Dylan had ordered an attack instead of Baneful Bunker, Toxapex would have taken the full brunt of Earthquake.

Giovanni was as cunning as ever.

"Tch… this won't be easy."

Dylan smirked, eyes gleaming with excitement.

Giovanni simply chuckled.

"Did you think defeating my Golem would be simple?"

His deep voice carried amusement.

"If it was that easy, I wouldn't be the strongest man in Kanto."

He tilted his head slightly, his smirk widening.

"This battle isn't just about winning, Dylan."

His dark gaze bore into Dylan's.

"I want to see if you're worthy of standing at the very top."

"So don't disappoint me."

To be continued…

---
