# 257: Chapter 258: Excuse me?

Dylan carefully analyzed his next move. Toxapex was a defensive counter-attacker, which meant if Giovanni refused to engage directly, things could get tricky.

"Stone Edge."

Giovanni gave the command.

Golem let out a deep growl, forming sharp, jagged stones in front of itself.

"Scald." Dylan didn't hesitate.

Toxapex raised its tentacle-like arms, gathering blue energy—a powerful Water-type attack.

"Dodge." Giovanni's deep eyes flickered slightly.

He didn't even need to think about it—he knew Dylan wasn't going to simply trade blows. Instead, Dylan would aim to misalign the attack trajectories, attempting a damage trade.

Golem was a high Attack Pokémon, while Toxapex couldn't afford a direct hit. Even though Water-types countered Rock, a Stone Edge from Golem would absolutely smash through Scald.

Dylan was clearly aiming to stagger the attacks, taking some damage while ensuring Golem suffered far more due to its quadruple Water weakness.

Golem aborted its Stone Edge attack, shifting its body to dodge the incoming Scald. As it moved, the Toxic Spikes activated once more, its poison seeping further into Golem's body.

"Impressive, Dylan. Against you, a standard battle approach won't gain much ground," Giovanni admitted. The battle had barely begun, yet it was already clear how well they both understood each other's tactics.

"Are you finally going to attack, then?" Dylan smirked.

"As you wish."

Giovanni's lips curved slightly. It was time to get a little more aggressive.

"Golem—Dig!"

Golem lowered its head, burrowing swiftly into the battlefield. Toxapex had no real counter to Dig.

"Iron Defense."

Dylan quickly adjusted his strategy.

Toxapex's body shimmered with a metallic sheen, boosting its Defense by two stages.

The ground beneath it suddenly cracked, and Golem burst out, slamming into Toxapex with full force—a Ground-type attack against a Poison-type!

Toxapex let out a pained cry, clearly feeling the super-effective hit.

But the problems didn't stop there.

As it was knocked backward, it collided with Stealth Rock, causing an explosion of sharp stone shards.

Giovanni had planned this perfectly—Dig's impact had pushed Toxapex right into the Stealth Rock trap!

"Earthquake!"

"Scald!"

Giovanni quickly followed up, while Dylan countered immediately.

As Golem raised its foot, a dark purple glow spread across its body—the poison damage from Toxic Spikes was intensifying!

Golem groaned, but endured it, slamming the ground hard—triggering a massive Earthquake!

The ground shook violently, sending shockwaves toward Toxapex.

At the same time, Toxapex hurled its Scald attack, launching a pressurized jet of boiling water straight at Golem.

Both attacks landed.

Toxapex shuddered in pain from the Earthquake, its defense barely holding up.

But Golem let out a deep roar, stumbling backward from the Scald.

The Water-type move was brutally effective, dealing massive damage despite Golem's bulk.

"Ha… not bad."

Giovanni chuckled. Though Golem had taken heavy damage, it had successfully dealt significant blows in return.

But Golem had now suffered three rounds of poison damage, meaning its time was limited.

"This is insane…"

Lillie watched in awe from the stands.

She knew Toxapex was one of Dylan's strongest defensive Pokémon, and yet, Giovanni's Golem was still forcing such an intense battle.

"Dig again!"

Giovanni opted for a repeat strategy—Dig was incredibly effective against Pokémon that lacked Earthquake resistance.

"Come back, Toxapex."

Dylan swiftly returned his Pokémon—there was no point staying in a battle where he had no good counterplay.

He grabbed a different Poké Ball.

"Salamence—let's go!"

A massive, winged dragon burst onto the battlefield, its wings stretching wide as it let out a mighty roar.

The moment it emerged, a Stealth Rock shard detonated, striking Salamence head-on.

Super effective!

Flying-types were weak to Rock, and Salamence winced from the impact.

But now Golem's Dig attack had nowhere to land—with Salamence airborne, Golem had no choice but to surface back up.

"Return," Giovanni said calmly, recalling Golem.

Fighting a Salamence in the air was a losing battle for Golem, especially when Salamence had Water-type attacks.

Giovanni retrieved a new Poké Ball.

"Gliscor—let's go!"

A large, bat-like scorpion materialized in the air.

It hovered menacingly, its sharp pincers clenching, ready for combat.

Rotom Pokédex beeped, displaying its stats.

Gliscor, Ground/Flying Type, Fang Scorpion PokémonAbility: Hyper CutterMoves: Swords Dance, Crabhammer, Earthquake, Night Slash, Acrobatics, Quick Attack, Ice Fang…

"This Gliscor is strong, too!"

Lillie's eyes widened as she read the Pokédex entry.

Dylan immediately called out, "Defog!"

Salamence roared, flapping its massive wings.

A powerful gust of wind swept through the battlefield, clearing away the Stealth Rock and Toxic Spikes.

The hazards were completely removed.

A strong wind buffeted Gliscor, but it barely reacted—its gaze remained locked on Salamence.

"We'll need to watch out for Ice Fang."

Dylan frowned slightly.

He owned a Gliscor himself, so he knew how it worked.

Gliscor had access to Ice Fang, and Giovanni wouldn't hesitate to use it against Salamence.

"Hurricane!"

Salamence roared again, whipping up a massive tornado of wind, sending it straight toward Gliscor.

"Quick Attack."

Giovanni's eyes gleamed.

A burst of white light flared from behind Gliscor as it suddenly accelerated downward, skimming just above the ground.

With its Quick Attack boost, Gliscor dodged the Hurricane by mere inches before swiftly soaring back up.

Now, it was rapidly closing the distance to Salamence.

"Stone Edge!"

Salamence flapped its wings and pulled back, summoning sharp, floating stone blades in front of itself.

Stone Edge shot forward at high speed, but Gliscor once again demonstrated its incredible gliding technique, shifting sideways midair to dodge the incoming attack.

At this moment, Gliscor had already closed the distance to Salamence.

"Ice Fang!"

Gliscor bared its fangs, an eerie frosty mist spilling from its mouth as it lunged at Salamence.

An Ice-type move—quadruple effective!

"Giovanni, I know Salamence is 4x weak to Ice, but you do realize that Gliscor's raw power is nowhere near Salamence's, right?" Dylan smirked.

"Salamence—Dragon Rush!"

Salamence fully spread its crimson wings, its body engulfed in pure, violent draconic energy.

A dark, dragon-shaped aura roared around it, exuding a terrifying killing intent.

Dragon Rush!

Giovanni's sharp eyes flickered for a moment. This battle aura-infused Dragon Rush was extraordinary.

Salamence crashed head-on into Gliscor, a thunderous explosion rippling through the battlefield.

Gliscor's Ice Fang had no chance—Salamence overpowered it completely and drove its body downward at terrifying speed.

Gliscor simply wasn't strong enough to match Salamence's raw force.

With another earth-shaking impact, Dragon Rush slammed Gliscor into the battlefield. The ground cracked and shattered, deep fissures splitting across the floor.

Gliscor let out a painful shriek.

Although Dragon Rush wasn't super effective, the sheer brute force behind it was overwhelming.

"Pin it down—use Scald!"

Dylan didn't hesitate to follow up.

Salamence stomped down heavily on Gliscor's chest, preventing it from escaping. A bright blue glow gathered in its mouth.

Gliscor struggled violently, but Salamence's physical power was too immense.

"It can't even bite back now!"

Lillie's eyes widened in realization.

Salamence had pinned Gliscor's chest, meaning no matter how much Gliscor struggled, it physically couldn't reach Salamence to bite it.

Matori glanced at Lillie before shifting her gaze back to the battlefield, her brows furrowing slightly.

She hadn't expected Giovanni to be on the back foot this early.

This Dylan—he was this strong?

Giovanni's expression remained unreadable, his dark eyes staring ahead.

Salamence's Scald attack blasted downward, drenching Gliscor in scalding hot water.

Gliscor shrieked in agony, the steam rising from its body as the attack burned into its exoskeleton.

"Use Toxic."

Giovanni's voice was low and cold.

Dylan's brow arched slightly, his gaze locking onto Gliscor's long, segmented tail.

Despite the searing pain of Scald, Gliscor's tail still moved, shimmering with an ominous purple glow.

A split second later, the poisoned stinger plunged into Salamence's abdomen.

Toxic!

Dark purple toxins spread instantly, poison seeping through Salamence's body.

A sinister energy flashed across Salamence's scales, the first signs of toxic poisoning appearing.

Salamence roared in pain, staggering backward and flapping its wings to retreat.

It was badly poisoned!

"Scald!"

Dylan reacted instantly.

Salamence, ignoring the pain, charged up another powerful water jet and fired it straight at Gliscor.

"Quick Attack."

Giovanni's cold smirk sent a chill down Dylan's spine.

Gliscor forced itself forward, moving at blinding speed, narrowly dodging the Scald before taking to the skies once more.

Salamence and Gliscor once again faced off in midair.

But now, their conditions were vastly different.

Gliscor was heavily injured, clearly weakened from Dragon Rush and Scald.

But Salamence was now poisoned—and not just regular poison, but Toxic poison.

Toxic damage escalated over time, meaning Salamence was now racing against the clock.

"So, you planned this from the start, Giovanni?"

Dylan loosened his collar slightly, his eyes narrowing.

"I honestly didn't expect you to use Toxic."

Giovanni was supposed to be a Ground-type specialist, yet he had just landed a critical hit with a status move?

Dylan had overestimated Giovanni's sense of fair play.

He knew Giovanni was ruthless in underground battles, but this was supposed to be a formal match.

"Ground-type moves don't work against Salamence. Naturally, I had to come up with other solutions," Giovanni replied with an almost bored tone.

"And honestly? I learned this from watching you, Dylan."

Giovanni chuckled, his voice low and magnetic, exuding his usual charisma.

"I've watched many of your battles. They were… insightful."

"Dylan, your Salamence is a powerhouse, no doubt about it. If I fought you head-on, I'd need to waste too much effort. However…"

"No matter how strong the opponent is, once they're hit by Toxic, their fate is sealed."

Giovanni's hands remained tucked in his pockets, his gaze dark and calculating.

"After all, wasn't it you who said that once a Pokémon is badly poisoned, it's nothing more than a candle flickering in the wind?"

Dylan gave Giovanni a deadpan stare.

"…Excuse me?"

# 258: Chapter 259: Fun

"No matter how strong an opponent is, once they're poisoned by Toxic, they become nothing more than a flickering candle in the wind."

That was something Dylan had said himself in the past.

But he never expected someone to throw it back at him like this.

And of all people, it had to be Giovanni.

Well, it made some sense—Giovanni's ace Pokémon included Beedrill, which was Poison-type.

But still… something about this felt off.

"You seem troubled," Giovanni noted, catching Dylan's expression. His smirk deepened.

"You're not wrong, but, Giovanni—your Gliscor has to survive long enough to see my Salamence fall first," Dylan grinned.

"Salamence—Fire Spin!"

Dylan's voice rang out.

With Toxic taking effect, there was no time to waste.

Salamence let out a thunderous roar, a massive vortex of fire spiraling from its mouth, surging toward Gliscor.

"Quick Attack."

Giovanni reacted immediately.

A burst of white energy flared from Gliscor's body as it accelerated, narrowly avoiding the fiery tornado.

"If you think you can run forever—Heat Wave!"

Salamence flapped its wings hard, summoning a blazing red wind that spread across the battlefield.

This time, the area of effect was much larger than Fire Spin!

"Gliscor."

Giovanni's voice was low.

Gliscor dove downward, attempting to escape the searing gusts while also closing the gap between itself and Salamence.

Salamence hovered in place, Heat Wave still raging.

Then—

A dark purple glow flashed across its body.

Toxic's second wave of damage had kicked in.

Salamence winced, its wings faltering for just a second.

That moment was all Gliscor needed.

"Quick Attack!"

Gliscor accelerated, slamming into Salamence's side.

Salamence grunted, then furiously roared in retaliation.

"Scald!"

Dylan countered instantly—if Gliscor wanted to fight up close, then so be it.

A bright blue glow surged in Salamence's mouth.

"Protect!"

A green energy shield materialized just in time, blocking the powerful water blast.

Salamence's fierce eyes locked onto the shimmering barrier, waiting for it to fade.

The moment the shield dispersed—

"Dragon Claw!"

"Acrobatics!"

Salamence's sharp claws tore through the air, while Gliscor spun midair, its bat-like wings slicing downward.

The two Pokémon clashed fiercely, neither willing to back down.

But then—

A third wave of Toxic poison pulsed through Salamence's body.

Salamence stiffened in pain, its attack faltering.

Gliscor seized the opportunity, twisting midair and slamming its long scorpion tail against Salamence.

BAM!

Salamence's body lurched downward, barely managing to stay airborne.

"Ice Fang!"

Giovanni didn't hesitate.

Gliscor's fangs gleamed with a cold, blue frost as it lunged straight at Salamence's exposed neck.

"I won't let you—Flamethrower!"

Dylan called out urgently.

Salamence forced itself to endure the pain, opening its mouth and releasing a blazing inferno.

Giovanni's sharp eyes narrowed.

He had underestimated Salamence's sheer endurance.

BOOM!

The flames collided directly with Gliscor, engulfing it in a fiery explosion.

Gliscor shrieked as it was blown backward.

"Scald!"

A massive jet of scalding water followed immediately after.

"Quick Attack!"

Gliscor barely stabilized itself midair, dodging just in time.

Both Pokémon faced off once again, breathing heavily.

But now, they were in vastly different conditions.

Gliscor was severely weakened, barely able to keep itself steady.

But Salamence was now experiencing Toxic's fourth wave of damage.

Dylan could already see it—Salamence was at its limit.

Lillie, watching anxiously from the stands, clasped her hands together.

"Hold on, Salamence…!"

But then—

Gliscor burst forward, slamming into Salamence's neck with Quick Attack.

Salamence growled in pain.

The fourth Toxic pulse hit.

It was unbearable.

"Scald!"

"Ice Fang!"

Dylan and Giovanni commanded simultaneously.

Salamence launched one final Scald, its body shaking from the poison.

At the same time, Gliscor sank its frozen fangs into Salamence's neck, an icy mist spreading instantly.

The two Pokémon crashed to the ground simultaneously.

THUD.

A heavy silence filled the Gym.

Matori walked forward, adjusting her glasses.

"Salamence and Gliscor are both unable to battle."

She paused for a moment, then glanced sideways at Dylan.

This guy… is seriously strong.

She had heard plenty of rumors about Rota Gym and Dylan, especially since he was responsible for taking down so many Team Rocket officers.

But she never expected him to be able to go toe-to-toe with Giovanni.

"They actually tied…"

Lillie pursed her lips.

Her gaze shifted toward Giovanni.

She had always heard he was the strongest Gym Leader in Kanto, but this level of skill was something else.

Her brother, Dylan, had easily defeated Wallace, the Hoenn Champion.

But here, against Giovanni, he was locked in an all-out battle.

And his Salamence was even stronger than the Pokémon he used against Wallace.

Which meant…

"Would Giovanni be able to completely destroy Wallace?"

Lillie frowned slightly.

"How many Wallaces… would it take to match one Giovanni?"

If Giovanni could battle Dylan this evenly, and considering Dylan had easily swept Wallace, then…

Lillie's eyes widened as she did the math.

Would that mean Giovanni was as strong as five Wallaces?

Her mind reeled at the thought.

If that logic was correct, then Giovanni wasn't just a Gym Leader—he was a champion-level powerhouse!

And there was no flaw in that reasoning.

Across the battlefield, Giovanni recalled his fainted Gliscor, staring at the Poké Ball for a long moment.

His expression remained unreadable—was he thinking about something?

"...It seems I underestimated you, Dylan."

After a long silence, Giovanni let out a chuckle, lifting his head to meet Dylan's gaze.

"Shouldn't I be saying that to you?" Dylan smirked.

"Giovanni, with this level of strength, are you really just a Gym Leader?"

Dylan tilted his head slightly, studying the man in the black, custom-tailored suit standing across from him.

Even if Dylan didn't already know Giovanni's true identity from past events, his sheer battle presence was enough to make anyone question who he really was.

Dylan's Salamence was one of his top-tier Pokémon, ranked just below his legendary-tier team.

And yet, it had just fainted in a close fight.

"I've told you before—I am the strongest man in Kanto."

Giovanni, still with one hand in his pocket, let out a quiet laugh before retrieving his third Poké Ball.

"As for everything else… let's finish this battle first, shall we?"

"Of course."

Dylan nodded, locking eyes with Giovanni before tossing his next Poké Ball.

In a flash of white light, a massive, armored Pokémon slammed onto the battlefield, letting out a deep, rumbling growl.

Rhyperior.

Across from it, a sleek, feline Pokémon gracefully straightened its posture, raising its arms with a sense of elegant arrogance.

Its gem-like eyes glinted proudly—Meowscarada.

"Tch—lucky draw, huh?"

Dylan clicked his tongue.

Rhyperior was quadruple weak to both Water and Grass. Even with Solid Rock reducing super-effective damage, it was still a 3x weakness.

"Not switching?" Dylan smirked.

"Typing advantages are just numbers," Giovanni said smoothly, completely unfazed.

Ground-type Gym Leaders had spent their whole careers fighting against type disadvantages—this was nothing new.

Dylan narrowed his eyes.

"Not bad, Giovanni. Not bad at all."

"Dig."

Giovanni wasted no time.

Rhyperior lowered its head, burrowing into the battlefield with impressive speed.

With its naturally slow movement, it wouldn't be able to catch Meowscarada normally—but Dig allowed it to close the distance from underground.

"Sucker Punch!"

Meowscarada's eyes flashed darkly, and in an instant, its green, nimble body lunged forward, vanishing into a shadowy blur.

A priority move—Sucker Punch!

But—

Rhyperior was already underground.

Before Meowscarada could land the hit, its target had disappeared.

"...Fast."

Dylan's brows lifted slightly.

For such a massive Pokémon, that Dig execution was incredibly practiced.

Even Meowscarada's speed-based Sucker Punch couldn't catch it in time.

"Meowscarada, stay alert."

"Meow~!"

The feline Pokémon stood motionless, ears twitching, emerald eyes narrowing as it scanned the ground.

Then—

The earth shifted slightly beneath its feet.

Instantly, Meowscarada flipped backward, leaping away.

But—

That was a feint.

Dylan's eyes narrowed.

The real Dig strike was waiting just behind the landing spot.

"Arm Thrust!"

The ground exploded open, and Rhyperior burst upward, aiming a crushing double-fisted strike at the airborne Meowscarada.

"Energy Ball!"

Meowscarada twisted midair, stretching out a paw as a glowing green sphere materialized in its palm.

At point-blank range, the Energy Ball shot forward.

BOOM!

The two attacks collided—but Rhyperior's sheer power crushed through Energy Ball, its fists slamming into Meowscarada's body.

The feline Pokémon let out a pained cry, its light frame sent flying.

But mid-fall, it gracefully twisted its body, landing on one hand and sliding backward, regaining balance.

Meowscarada's gem-like eyes burned with cold determination as it locked onto Rhyperior.

It had taken the hit, but it wasn't down yet.

Dylan rubbed his chin.

Rhyperior's Attack is no joke.

Even with Energy Ball dampening the damage, Arm Thrust still hit like a truck.

This Rhyperior was clearly one of Giovanni's top-tier powerhouses.

Which made this battle even more exciting.

Dylan licked his lips slightly.

This… this was fun.

# 259: Chapter 260: Bug

On the battlefield, Meowscarada and Rhyperior faced off.

"Earthquake!" Giovanni wasted no time.

Rhyperior lifted its massive, wrecking-ball-like tail and slammed it into the ground with full force.

BOOM!

A devastating shockwave of raw seismic energy erupted from the battlefield, rippling toward Meowscarada.

"Exchange damage? No… something's off."

Dylan narrowed his eyes.

Meowscarada had resistance to Earthquake, but its Grass-type attacks dealt massive damage to Rhyperior.

Logically, Dylan could have just ordered a Grass move, taking Earthquake in exchange for a finishing blow.

But Giovanni wasn't the type to ignore something that obvious.

Something wasn't right.

"Sucker Punch!"

Meowscarada's eyes gleamed darkly, and in a flash, it dashed toward Rhyperior like a shadowy blur.

As the Earthquake waves crashed through the battlefield, Meowscarada leaped into the air, dodging the impact zone.

Giovanni frowned slightly—a missed opportunity.

If Dylan had gone for a direct attack, he could have baited him into a trap, using Dig to escape and get a free hit.

Rhyperior was a master of underground combat—its Dig execution was extremely fast.

But now that the moment was lost—

BAM!

Meowscarada's Sucker Punch slammed into Rhyperior's forehead, but the massive Pokémon barely staggered an inch.

As expected, its monstrous Defense made direct attacks almost useless.

"Heat Crash!"

Rhyperior roared, its body suddenly bursting into flames.

A Fire-type version of Heavy Slam!

The heavier the user compared to its opponent, the stronger the attack—and Rhyperior was significantly heavier than Meowscarada.

A wall of flames surged forward, the massive Pokémon charging like a meteor of destruction.

"Dodge!"

Meowscarada effortlessly twisted its body, sidestepping the attack with fluid elegance.

It was far too agile for a lumbering tank like Rhyperior.

Giovanni wasn't surprised.

It was worth trying, but Meowscarada's speed was simply too overwhelming.

"Energy Ball!"

Meowscarada immediately countered, summoning a pulsating green orb of energy and launching it at point-blank range.

BOOM!

The Energy Ball exploded against Rhyperior's back, causing the massive Pokémon to stagger and growl in pain.

Dylan narrowed his eyes.

That reaction…

This Rhyperior had Solid Rock—a passive ability that reduced super-effective damage.

Without it, that Energy Ball would have done far more damage.

Giovanni remained calm.

"Stone Edge!"

Sharp, floating jagged rocks materialized around Rhyperior.

"Magical Leaf!"

Meowscarada whipped its arm, sending a swarm of glowing green leaves at Rhyperior.

Clang! Clang! Clang!

The Stone Edge easily smashed through the Magical Leaf attack, overpowering it as the remaining stone blades shot toward Meowscarada.

Meowscarada leaped gracefully, dodging the projectiles.

But—

When it looked back—

Rhyperior was gone.

A black hole of a Dig tunnel remained in its place.

"It's underground again… Stay sharp."

Meowscarada narrowed its eyes, ears twitching cautiously.

"Double Team."

Meowscarada split into eight afterimages, spreading out across the battlefield.

A classic evasion tactic—and unlike in the games, it worked visually in real battles.

With so many copies, an underground attacker would struggle to find the real target.

But—

Suddenly, the ground trembled beneath one of the Meowscarada clones.

Dylan's brow twitched—

This fast?!

In the blink of an eye—

Rhyperior burst from the earth, targeting the correct Meowscarada in an instant.

"Superpower!"

Rhyperior's fist glowed red-hot, its entire body radiating pure brute force.

If that landed, Meowscarada was finished!

"Acrobatics!"

Meowscarada's white aura flared as it suddenly flipped midair, using the power of Acrobatics to boost its evasive maneuver.

Rhyperior's Superpower punch just barely missed, smashing into the ground with a thunderous crash.

Dylan exhaled slightly.

That was too close.

Giovanni smirked.

"You should've known, Dylan."

"Rhyperior may be slow, but underground, it doesn't need speed—it senses pressure."

"Your Double Team illusions didn't have real mass. Rhyperior could immediately tell which one was real."

Dylan's eye twitched.

That was the same trick he used when controlling Garchomp underground.

No wonder Giovanni saw through it.

"Tch."

"Leaf Storm!"

Meowscarada's body shone bright green, a massive whirlwind of razor-sharp leaves bursting from its form.

BOOM!

The full-powered Grass-type attack engulfed Rhyperior, slashing through its rocky hide mercilessly.

Rhyperior bellowed in pain, the storm shredding through its defenses.

When the attack finally ended, Rhyperior was badly battered—but still standing.

Its deep, labored breathing filled the battlefield.

It wasn't down yet, but it was heavily weakened.

Dylan smirked.

"That ability of yours saved you—but you're at your limit now."

"Flower Trick!"

Meowscarada whipped its bouquet, sending a deadly red flower hurtling toward Rhyperior.

Still weakened from Leaf Storm, Rhyperior had no chance to dodge.

The moment it struck—

BOOM!

A massive explosion of petals and energy engulfed Rhyperior.

As the dust settled—

Rhyperior remained motionless, eyes shut.

"Rhyperior is unable to battle. Meowscarada wins."

Matori announced the result, adjusting her glasses slightly.

She gave Dylan a long sideways glance.

This guy… really is insane.

Even she had heard the rumors about Dylan and Rota Gym—after all, he was responsible for taking down so many Team Rocket officers.

But actually seeing him fight Giovanni on equal ground?

That was unreal.

"Yes!"

Lillie clenched her fists in excitement.

Her brother had won the round.

"An impressive Meowscarada."

Giovanni recalled Rhyperior, offering a nod of approval.

That last dodge into Acrobatics was perfectly executed—if it had been even a second slower, Rhyperior's Superpower would've ended the fight right there.

Not many trainers could react that quickly.

Meowscarada flicked its tail smugly, obviously enjoying the praise.

"Dylan, you may have won this round…"

Giovanni retrieved his fourth Poké Ball.

"But let's see how you handle this one."

The Poké Ball burst open—

Dugtrio!

On the battlefield, Dugtrio's three round heads bobbed slightly, each one subtly different in size and expression.

Beep—Scanning…

Dugtrio, Ground-type, Mole PokémonAbility: Arena TrapMoves: Fissure, Earthquake, Earth Power, Dig, Slash, Sucker Punch, Sand Tomb, Stomping Tantrum…

Lillie's eyes flicked to the Rotom Pokédex screen.

This Dugtrio was ridiculously strong!

And more importantly—

Arena Trap!

That meant Dylan couldn't switch out Meowscarada!

"Arena Trap, huh?" Dylan muttered, glancing at the fine grains of sand slowly swirling around Meowscarada's feet.

A classic Dugtrio strategy—lock the opponent in and wear them down.

But it wasn't a problem.

Because Meowscarada knew U-turn.

"Rock Slide."

Giovanni's calm command came immediately.

Dugtrio let out a sharp cry, summoning a barrage of large boulders that rained down toward Meowscarada.

"Sucker Punch!"

Meowscarada's eyes flashed dark, its sleek green body blurring as it lunged forward at lightning speed.

But—

Dugtrio vanished into the ground in an instant.

The attack missed.

Meowscarada landed lightly on the battlefield, eyes darting around warily.

Dugtrio had disappeared.

For Dugtrio, digging underground wasn't just a move—it was an instinct.

"Sand Tomb."

A smirk curled at Giovanni's lips.

The battlefield began to shift, the ground beneath Meowscarada slowly turning into a swirling vortex of sand.

A Ground-type trap move—it would continuously damage the target while restricting movement.

Meowscarada's eyes narrowed as it instinctively leapt backward—but the Sand Tomb kept expanding!

The swirling sands spread across the battlefield, pushing Meowscarada closer and closer to the edge.

In the very center—

Dugtrio poked its heads out briefly, shifting slightly before disappearing again.

"Dylan, what now?" Giovanni mused.

Dylan studied the battlefield.

That Dugtrio was playing dirty.

Its constant underground movement made it nearly impossible to target directly, and the expanding Sand Tomb was slowly backing Meowscarada into a corner.

"If I had Earthquake, this would be easy," Dylan muttered under his breath.

Earthquake could collapse the sand traps and flush Dugtrio out—but Meowscarada didn't know it.

Fine.

They'd force Dugtrio out another way.

"Frenzy Plant!"

Meowscarada's deep voice rumbled, its entire body glowing with intense green energy.

BOOM!

The battlefield trembled as massive thorned vines erupted from the ground, writhing and twisting like a storm of living blades.

One by one, the vines ripped through the swirling sands, shattering the Sand Tomb completely.

Dugtrio barely managed to surface, only to be met with a wall of oncoming vines.

A direct hit was imminent.

But Giovanni only smiled.

"As expected. Dugtrio, Fissure."

Dugtrio thrust its heads forward, its entire body shaking violently.

A deep, jagged crack ripped through the battlefield, heading straight for the stunned Meowscarada.

A one-hit KO move in the games—but in reality, Fissure was just an overwhelmingly powerful Ground-type attack.

Meowscarada couldn't move.

Not because of Sand Tomb—but because of Frenzy Plant's cooldown.

BOOM!

The ground erupted, and Meowscarada let out a cry of pain, its body slammed with raw seismic force.

When the dust settled—

Meowscarada lay motionless.

"Meowscarada is unable to battle. Dugtrio wins."

Matori pushed up her glasses, glancing sideways at Dylan.

"That Dugtrio…" Lillie frowned slightly.

This wasn't a normal Dugtrio.

Dylan remained silent for a moment before recalling Meowscarada.

"You did great."

Then, he looked up at Giovanni, smirking.

"Not bad. Not bad at all."

But—

How would Giovanni handle this?

Dylan plucked a Luxury Ball from his belt.

"Let me introduce you to something special."

The Poké Ball opened with a brilliant flash, and a large, armored insectoid Pokémon landed in front of Dylan.

Its body was covered in sleek, violet plating, and a large, high-tech cannon rested on its back.

Its glowing red eyes burned coldly, radiating an unnerving mechanical precision.

Genesect.

The extinct hunter revived by science.

One of the Genesect that Colress had given him—not the prototype, but still one of Dylan's most powerful Pokémon.

Beep—Scanning…

Genesect, Bug/Steel Type, Ancient PokémonAbility: DownloadMoves: Techno Blast (Water Drive), Lock-On, Zap Cannon, Simple Beam, X-Scissor, Iron Head…

Description: Feared as the strongest hunter of the ancient world, Genesect ruled the planet 300 million years ago before its kind went extinct. It has since been revived and modified by Team Plasma.

"Genesect?"

Giovanni's eyes narrowed slightly.

He had never seen this Pokémon before.

"That's right—Genesect."

Dylan's smile widened.

"It also has another name."

Giovanni watched as the mechanical predator adjusted its stance, its cannon humming to life.

Dylan's voice was casual, but carried a hint of amusement.

"The Doomsday Bug."

---

---

---

# 260: Chapter 261: Something’s Off With Him!

"Doomsday Bug?"

Giovanni's eyes narrowed slightly at the name.

Just from the sound of it, he knew—this wasn't an ordinary Pokémon.

"So, Giovanni, you'd better be careful," Dylan said with a casual grin. "Genesect is a Mythical Pokémon—Bug and Steel-type."

He dropped that information deliberately.

He didn't believe in taking advantage of an information gap—that wasn't his style.

"Mythical Pokémon, huh?"

Giovanni nodded slowly. A Legendary-tier Pokémon, and Bug-Steel at that—a near-perfect type combination with no weaknesses except Fire.

This was going to be tricky.

"Sand Tomb."

Dugtrio's three heads trembled, and the ground beneath Genesect began swirling like quicksand.

"Genesect."

Genesect's red eyes flickered eerily, scanning Dugtrio's movements.

Download activated—Genesect's Attack increased!

Because Dugtrio's Defense was lower than its Special Defense, Download automatically boosted Genesect's physical power.

The robotic predator didn't hesitate—with a burst of energy, it lifted off the ground.

"Flying capabilities?" Giovanni murmured.

This… just got worse.

He hated dealing with Flying Pokémon.

As a Ground-type specialist, airborne opponents were always a problem.

Especially ones that hit this hard from a distance.

"Techno Blast!"

Genesect's cannon hummed to life, blue energy gathering rapidly.

A highly concentrated water-based beam erupted from its cannon, hurtling straight at Dugtrio.

Giovanni's response was immediate.

"Dugtrio, Dig!"

Dugtrio vanished underground, barely dodging the blast.

BOOM!

The battlefield shook violently, water exploding across the field, completely washing away the Sand Tomb trap.

Giovanni frowned slightly.

That wasn't a normal Water-type move.

Genesect's Techno Blast… packed serious power.

As the battlefield settled, Dugtrio poked its heads up from underground, cautiously surveying the situation.

"...Return," Giovanni said, recalling Dugtrio.

No point forcing a losing fight—Dugtrio had no effective way to counter a Flying, long-range attacker like Genesect.

"Not bad," Giovanni admitted. "I expected no less from a Mythical Pokémon."

"So, how will you deal with it, Giovanni?" Dylan asked, eyes flashing with curiosity.

What would Giovanni send out next?

Nidoking?

That was possible. Giovanni was known for using Nidoking and Nidoqueen as his powerhouses.

But—

Giovanni suddenly chuckled.

"Dylan, did you know?"

His gaze turned nostalgic, fingers idly spinning his next Poké Ball.

"I was born in Viridian Forest—just like you."

Dylan's smirk faded slightly.

But Lillie's reaction was far more extreme—her eyes widened in shock.

Because she saw it.

The faint green glow on Giovanni's hand.

That wasn't Pokémon energy.

That was his own power.

Giovanni had… Viridian Aura?!

This was never mentioned anywhere!

Her thoughts raced.

Giovanni… chosen by Viridian Forest?

No one had ever spoken of this before—not even rumors.

"Viridian Aura," Dylan said, eyes narrowing slightly. "Giovanni, that's something you've never told anyone."

Giovanni smirked, the green glow fading from his hand.

"I never cared for it," he said calmly.

Ever since he had become the leader of Team Rocket, he had barely used this ability.

Viridian Aura represented harmony with nature.

But Giovanni had long since abandoned that path.

It was ironic—he sought to control the world through money and Pokémon, yet nature had once chosen him.

"Dylan," Giovanni said, his voice lower now, more serious.

"You and I are more alike than you think."

His eyes gleamed with something almost dangerous.

"Unlike Lance."

A sharp sneer curled at his lips.

"Lance is too extreme. He won't end well."

Lillie froze.

This…

That's not something a Gym Leader should say.

She was certain of it now.

Something was seriously wrong with Giovanni.

Dylan's gaze remained calm.

"If Lance heard you say that, he wouldn't be happy, Giovanni."

"But you wouldn't care, would you?" Giovanni murmured.

He leaned forward slightly.

"Dylan, tell me… do you truly like the Pokémon League?"

Silence.

Dylan didn't answer right away.

Finally, he exhaled.

"It's… fine, I suppose."

"I don't hate it. The world would be a mess without it."

He meant it.

Dylan wasn't some noble, self-sacrificing hero.

But he knew that without the Pokémon League, things would descend into chaos.

Sure, the League had its corrupt officials.

But people like Charles Goodshow, Steven, Cynthia, Alder, and even Wallace—they were genuinely trying to do good.

And then there was Lance.

Lance hated corruption with every fiber of his being.

He wanted a world where justice was absolute.

But Giovanni was right about one thing—

Lance was too extreme.

His worldview was… dangerously rigid.

People who refused to bend eventually broke.

"You're not wrong," Giovanni murmured, watching Dylan carefully.

"But we've talked enough."

He finally lifted his Poké Ball.

"It's time for my fifth Pokémon."

He tossed it.

BZZZZZZZZT!

A familiar buzzing filled the air.

A yellow insectoid Pokémon hovered before Giovanni, its long, twin stingers gleaming sharply.

"...What?"

Lillie blinked.

"A… Beedrill?"

But something was off.

Dylan's gaze sharpened slightly.

He recognized this Beedrill.

He had seen it once before—deep underground in Celadon City.

It was this Beedrill that had swooped in and rescued Archer and Proton from him.

And now, it was standing before him again.

"Huh," Dylan said, tilting his head. "Funny running into you again."

Giovanni smiled faintly.

"...Shall we finish this battle first?"

Dylan chuckled.

"Sure."

"This was my first Pokémon. Back then, I was still young when I encountered it as a Weedle in Viridian Forest."

Giovanni spoke slowly. Every trainer tends to have a special attachment to their first Pokémon, and he was no exception.

"All these years, it has remained my most loyal and powerful partner. Dylan, its strength is beyond comparison to the Golem and the others I used earlier."

Beedrill's wings vibrated gently, its red eyes locking onto Genesect.

"So, Giovanni, you were once a Bug Catcher too?"

Dylan sized up the Beedrill and teased Giovanni.

"Heh… In a sense, I suppose I was."

Giovanni was slightly surprised, but after a brief pause, he chuckled and nodded.

After all, his first Pokémon was a Weedle—calling him a Bug Catcher back then wasn't wrong at all.

"Well, I'll have to test its strength myself."

Dylan glanced at Giovanni standing behind Beedrill. This Beedrill was only his fifth Pokémon? Then what was Giovanni's final Pokémon?

Or had he sent out his ace Beedrill early just to counter Genesect?

But Dylan felt that wasn't the case. Judging by Giovanni's approach today, he was clearly here to settle things with him.

Giovanni's final Pokémon… seemed obvious now.

This was getting interesting!

"Genesect, Flamethrower!"

Dylan's eyes lit up with excitement—this was Giovanni's ace, Beedrill!

He knew that if he used Reshiram, he could defeat Giovanni. But he also believed that even without Necrozma or any Legendary Pokémon, he still wouldn't lose!

After all, he was practically cheating!

Genesect let out a low hum, and a scorching flame shot toward Beedrill.

"Poison Jab." Giovanni gave his command calmly.

Beedrill raised its right lance, a deep purple glow illuminating its tip.

As the flames approached, Beedrill thrust its arm forward, executing a precise and deadly Poison Jab!

Bang!

The Flamethrower stalled for a moment before shattering into pieces—completely neutralized by Poison Jab. Beedrill's execution was swift, precise, and ruthless!

"Huh?" Lillie was stunned.

What was with this Beedrill? That was a Flamethrower from Genesect! The power gap didn't make any sense.

"Brick Break!" Giovanni's lips curled into a sinister grin.

Beedrill's wings fluttered rapidly as it charged at Genesect at high speed.

"Zen Headbutt!" Dylan reacted immediately.

This Beedrill's strength was insane—it wasn't just a matter of high level. Compared to a regular Beedrill, Giovanni's was on a whole different tier.

It made sense—this was the Pokémon Giovanni had raised since childhood. He had poured his heart and soul into it.

Genesect showed no fear as it met Beedrill head-on, a colorful aura forming around its head.

A Psychic-type Zen Headbutt!

But Beedrill had no intention of dodging. It raised its right arm and swung down hard—

A Fighting-type Brick Break!

Boom!

The two moves clashed violently, sending a tangible shockwave rippling outward from the point of impact.

"What incredible power."

Seeing that Beedrill hadn't immediately overpowered Genesect, Giovanni narrowed his eyes slightly.

"But Beedrill has two arms."

Beedrill raised its left lance and swung it heavily at Genesect.

"Dodge!"

Genesect abandoned the struggle and quickly retreated to evade the strike.

"Thunderbolt!" Dylan immediately countered, certain that Beedrill would pursue.

Sparks crackled around Genesect, and sure enough, Beedrill was already lunging forward with its lance raised.

"Pursuit!"

Electricity surged forth, striking Beedrill directly and halting its advance.

But in the next instant, Beedrill powered through the Thunderbolt, closing in and driving its dark-glowing lance into Genesect's chest.

A vicious Pursuit attack!

Genesect let out a cry of pain as it was blasted backward like a cannonball, crashing hard into the ground, kicking up a cloud of dust.

"That Beedrill is insanely strong!"

Watching Beedrill dominate Genesect, Lillie gasped in disbelief. Genesect was a Mythical Pokémon that her brother had fully trained.

And yet, it was being overpowered by a Beedrill? Who would believe that?!

"Not even a single advantage…"

Dylan gazed at the Beedrill hovering in the air. Its power and speed were exceptional, and its special defense seemed remarkable as well.

Then again, that was expected—even normal Beedrill had similar traits, but Giovanni's Beedrill was far superior.

Still, it wasn't unbeatable. The fact that Genesect had been able to hold its ground proved that much.

Once he defeated Beedrill, Giovanni's final Pokémon would finally be revealed.

(End of Chapter)

---

---

---

# 261: Chapter 262: Giovanni’s Final Trump Card!

Dylan pondered how to defeat this Beedrill.

"Pursuit!"

Giovanni commanded another attack.

Beedrill's wings fluttered rapidly as it shot toward the grounded Genesect like a lightning bolt. Its speed was astonishing.

"Shadow Claw!" Dylan didn't hesitate.

Retreating wasn't an option. Beedrill's speed and explosive power were overwhelming—backing away would only make its pursuit even fiercer, leaving no escape.

And Genesect wasn't a defensive Pokémon to begin with.

Its claws shimmered with a sharp, eerie glow as it met Beedrill head-on—an incoming Ghost-type Shadow Claw!

Boom!

Pursuit and Shadow Claw collided with a violent shockwave, sending ripples through the air. It was clear that Beedrill's lance was pressing down on Genesect's claws.

Then, in the next instant, Beedrill's other lance cut through the air, thrusting straight for Genesect.

Another Pursuit!

Genesect quickly dodged to the side, countering with its second Shadow Claw.

Beedrill pulled back slightly to evade, then immediately closed the distance again.

The two Pokémon clashed midair in an intense melee, weaving through the air in a breathtaking exchange of attacks.

The sounds of moves colliding and energy bursting filled the battlefield. Lillie could hardly keep up with the sheer speed of the battle.

Then—

Boom!

Genesect's Shadow Claw was parried, and Beedrill's Pursuit struck it directly in the chest.

With a cry of pain, Genesect plummeted, crashing hard onto the ground. It lifted its head, breathing heavily as it stared at the Beedrill hovering above.

That prolonged high-speed combat had drained both its stamina and focus. And in the end, it had fallen behind.

"Looks like your Genesect isn't a match for my Beedrill, Dylan."

Giovanni grinned, his white teeth glinting ominously against his shadowed face. The overwhelming malice in his gaze was chilling.

"Pursuit!"

Beedrill paused briefly before rocketing toward the fallen Genesect once more.

"Quick Attack!"

Dylan commanded immediately.

Both Beedrill and Genesect were Bug-types, and since Genesect's Steel typing resisted Poison, Beedrill had been relying on Brick Break and Pursuit instead.

But Pursuit's effect meant that if the target had already taken damage, its power doubled.

A flash of white light burst from Genesect's body—it endured the pain and vanished from its spot just before Beedrill could strike.

With Quick Attack boosting its speed, Genesect dashed away, forcing Beedrill to twist midair and give chase.

"Thunderbolt!" Dylan's eyes gleamed.

Instead of Flamethrower, which was super effective against Beedrill, he opted for Thunderbolt—a move that could spread over a wider range.

Electricity crackled across Genesect's body before surging forward toward the oncoming Beedrill.

With no room to dodge sideways, Beedrill had no choice but to meet it head-on. It raised its right lance and swung—smashing through the lightning with another devastating Pursuit.

"You can't escape, Beedrill!"

Beedrill suddenly accelerated again, closing the gap between them.

"Blaze Kick!"

"Brick Break!"

Flames erupted around Genesect's right leg as it delivered a powerful flaming kick toward Beedrill.

Beedrill blocked with its right lance, taking the full force of the attack. But in the same motion, its other lance glowed white as it swung sideways—

Boom!

The Brick Break struck true, sending Genesect tumbling through the air once more.

Dylan narrowed his eyes slightly. This wasn't working.

"It's losing ground…"

Lillie frowned, watching Genesect continuously forced onto the defensive.

This Beedrill was beyond terrifying. As the battle dragged on, Genesect's disadvantage was growing more and more apparent.

She had never imagined a Beedrill could be raised to this level—it completely shattered her expectations.

"Pursuit!"

"Thunderbolt!"

Beedrill surged forward once again, its lances shrouded in dark energy.

Electricity sparked violently around Genesect as it fired another Thunderbolt, striking Beedrill mid-flight.

But Beedrill only hesitated for an instant.

Bang!

Its Pursuit landed, driving Genesect downward.

Boom!

The ground trembled as Genesect crashed, letting out a low, pained cry.

"Finish it—Brick Break!" Giovanni smirked, victory in sight.

Beedrill raised its right lance and swung it down toward Genesect.

"Final Gambit!"

Giovanni's expression stiffened.

"Beedrill!"

Beedrill immediately attempted to retreat, sensing the impending self-destructive attack.

Genesect's entire body glowed brilliantly white.

BOOM!

A deafening explosion erupted, unleashing a violent shockwave that forced everyone to shield their eyes.

From within the dissipating dust, Beedrill emerged—its body smoking, its breath ragged.

It had taken heavy damage from the Final Gambit.

As the dust settled, Genesect lay unconscious in a crater.

"Genesect is unable to battle. Beedrill wins."

Matori announced the result.

Dylan recalled his fainted Genesect, his gaze fixed on Beedrill. Despite its injuries, it had already steadied its breathing.

"A truly powerful Beedrill, Giovanni. Calling it the strongest Beedrill in the world wouldn't be an exaggeration."

Dylan clicked his tongue in admiration. He could only truly appreciate its strength now that he had faced it firsthand.

Genesect had exceptional power—it excelled in both melee and ranged combat, with virtually no weaknesses.

Yet Giovanni's Beedrill, using only Brick Break and Pursuit, had completely dominated the battle. Terrifying.

"A Mythical Pokémon using self-destruction… Dylan, you might be the first."

Giovanni chuckled lightly, not denying Dylan's praise. Instead, he marveled at Dylan's bold tactics.

He honestly hadn't expected Dylan to have Genesect use Final Gambit.

Of course, Giovanni hadn't even known Genesect could use that move—otherwise, he would have taken precautions.

"Well, my opponent is ridiculously strong," Dylan admitted with a grin, retrieving another Poké Ball.

"So, how do you plan to deal with my Beedrill?" Giovanni adjusted his tie, staring at Dylan.

"By defeating it head-on, of course!"

Dylan flipped the Poké Ball in his hand and threw it.

"Alright, Giovanni—this will be your next opponent."

"Marshadow! Prepare for battle!"

The luxury ball burst open, and a gray-black humanoid Pokémon emerged from the shadows, its hollow orange eyes locking onto Beedrill.

Marshadow.

"Marshadow? Another Mythical Pokémon."

Giovanni studied it carefully but wasn't particularly surprised that Dylan owned one.

Unless it was a Legendary Pokémon on the level of Ho-Oh, Giovanni didn't care much about most Legendaries or Mythicals.

The likes of Darkrai or the Articuno and Moltres that Sabrina and Koga had recently captured didn't interest him.

He was confident that his main Pokémon were just as strong as those so-called Legendary Pokémon.

After all, hadn't his Beedrill just defeated Genesect?

"That's right. Marshadow is a Ghost/Fighting type—so, Giovanni, I hope you'll enjoy experiencing its power." Dylan smirked.

"Ehhh… why does Marshadow look… chubbier?"

Lillie tilted her head in confusion.

She hadn't seen Marshadow in a while—it usually hid in Dylan's shadow and trained alone.

But now that she was seeing it again, she was sure of it.

Its waist looked… thicker?

Marshadow had always been small and adorable, giving off a cute and dopey vibe.

But now, it looked sturdier—almost like it had a little belly…

On the battlefield.

"Aerial Ace!" Giovanni made the first move.

Despite its dominance, Beedrill wasn't unscathed.

Giovanni had trained it for high-speed explosive attacks, but compared to its offense and speed, its defense was average at best.

Genesect's Thunderbolts were manageable, but that Final Gambit had been a heavy blow. Still, it wasn't enough to take Beedrill out of the fight.

Beedrill spun rapidly, gathering white energy before diving toward Marshadow—Aerial Ace, a Flying-type move!

"Shadow Punch!"

Marshadow clenched its fist, bent its knees, and leaped at high speed to meet Beedrill head-on.

A Ghost-type Shadow Punch! And with Technician, its power was even greater.

Boom!

Aerial Ace and Shadow Punch clashed, sending a shockwave rippling through the air. In the next instant, both Pokémon were knocked backward.

"This one's stronger than that Genesect."

Giovanni narrowed his eyes slightly.

This Marshadow was clearly more powerful than the Genesect from earlier. Just from its attack alone, it could already go toe-to-toe with his Beedrill.

"Pursuit!" Giovanni ordered again.

Beedrill's twin lances glowed black as it sped toward Marshadow.

"Spectral Thief!"

Marshadow's body melted into a shadow.

Beedrill swooped down, but its target had disappeared—it was the shadow itself!

As Beedrill neared the ground, ready to strike, the dark shadow beneath it suddenly expanded.

"Beedrill!"

Something was off. Giovanni immediately called out to it.

Beedrill flapped its wings to retreat, but at that moment, a surge of Ghostly energy erupted in a blinding pillar of darkness.

Dozens of eerie orange eyes flickered within the swirling mass of energy.

Even Lillie and Matori, who were watching, felt a chill—it was terrifying.

Spectral Thief!

Beedrill couldn't dodge in time and was engulfed by the attack. The dark energy washed over it relentlessly.

Gritting its teeth, Beedrill forced itself out of the attack's range, retreating midair.

"Aerial Ace!" Giovanni frowned. He hadn't fully understood how Spectral Thief worked until now.

But he knew one thing—once the attack ended, Marshadow would have to return to its physical form. That was the moment to strike.

Beedrill steadied itself, taking a breath before spinning into another Aerial Ace.

As the Spectral Thief energy dissipated, Marshadow reappeared from the shadows.

At the same time, Beedrill's attack was already upon it!

"Shadow Punch!"

Damn, that was fast. Dylan had to admit—Giovanni's battle instincts were incredible.

Marshadow clenched its fist, cloaked in ghostly energy, and swung at Beedrill.

"Beedrill!"

But just then, Beedrill suddenly tilted its body slightly—narrowly dodging the Shadow Punch.

A direct miss!

In the next instant, Beedrill spun around and slammed into Marshadow with Aerial Ace!

Marshadow cried out in pain—Flying-type moves were super effective against it.

"Still got some tricks up your sleeve." Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"Pursuit!"

"Fire Punch!"

Beedrill's right lance gleamed with dark energy as it struck toward the staggered Marshadow.

But Marshadow ducked low, narrowly avoiding the attack as Beedrill's lance pierced just above its head.

Taking the opportunity, Marshadow lunged forward, its right fist bursting into flames—Fire Punch!

Seeing this, Beedrill swung its other lance to intercept.

Giovanni frowned slightly—Marshadow's reaction speed was insane.

Boom!

Fire Punch smashed into Beedrill's waist!

A surge of red-hot flames exploded upon impact, and Beedrill let out a cry as it was blasted backward through the air.

Fire-type moves were super effective against Beedrill! That hit was devastating!

"We're winning this!!"

Lillie clutched her hands together nervously.

"Spectral Thief!"

Marshadow instantly transformed into a shadow, streaking after the airborne Beedrill.

A surge of dark energy shot into the sky, completely engulfing Beedrill before it even hit the ground.

Those eerie, hollow orange eyes reappeared, floating within the shadows.

BOOM!

Spectral Thief detonated with a deafening explosion. Darkness rippled outward, and Beedrill crashed to the ground, motionless.

Marshadow reformed from the shadows, standing quietly as it gazed at its fallen opponent.

Beedrill struggled, trying to lift its head, but pain wracked its body. It couldn't stand.

"Beedrill is unable to battle! Marshadow wins!"

Matori stepped forward, examined Beedrill, and announced the result.

Giovanni took a long, silent look at Beedrill's Poké Ball before recalling it. His deep eyes flickered with unreadable thoughts.

"…Impressive. Truly impressive."

After a moment, Giovanni lowered his head and chuckled softly, then looked at Dylan, clapping lightly.

"Dylan, you are truly strong."

His tone was full of genuine admiration.

"Since reaching Champion-level, my Beedrill has never been defeated. You are the first."

Giovanni understood—Beedrill was his strongest Pokémon, and yet, Dylan had bested it.

"Well, you also didn't know much about Genesect and Marshadow." Dylan smirked.

Genesect's Final Gambit had been crucial, and Spectral Thief had caught Giovanni off guard.

Besides, Giovanni's Beedrill still had untapped potential—he didn't have its Mega Stone yet. Once he did, its power would reach even greater heights.

"No, a loss is a loss."

Giovanni shook his head. Dylan had a point, but Giovanni wasn't one to make excuses for his own defeat.

Even if he had known about Genesect and Marshadow, whether Beedrill could have beaten both was still uncertain.

"Technically, our battle should end here, Dylan. Beedrill is my strongest Pokémon."

Giovanni gazed at Marshadow with a slight smile.

That Marshadow was incredibly powerful. Given Beedrill's defeat, his Rhyperior and Dugtrio wouldn't stand a chance against it.

"However, Dylan… You've probably guessed it. I have one last trump card."

"It's not my Pokémon, but right now, it's my ally. Let it be my final opponent against you."

Hands in his pockets, Giovanni spoke calmly.

Footsteps echoed in the silence.

A silver-white Pokémon emerged from behind Giovanni, its piercing psychic eyes locking onto Dylan.

Lillie's eyes widened in shock, the Pokémon's figure reflecting in her pupils.

She had seen this Pokémon before.

Back in the underground facility beneath Saffron City.

And its presence confirmed one thing—Giovanni was with Team Rocket!

Not just that—with Giovanni's strength, there was no doubt—

He was the LEADER of Team Rocket!

Wait… The Team Rocket boss was also a Gym Leader?!

The silver Pokémon came to a stop in front of Giovanni, staring directly at Marshadow.

"Dylan, this is my final fighter."

"Its name is… Mewtwo."

Giovanni's deep voice remained calm, but the pride in it was unmistakable.

Of course. Mewtwo.

Dylan raised an eyebrow.

"Tch… Now what should I use against Mewtwo?"

(End of Chapter)

New work is out! Go check it put!

# 262: Chapter 263: If Anyone’s Fighting Mewtwo, It Has to Be Me!

Mewtwo.

The legendary Pokémon created from Mew's genetic material.

Lillie remembered it clearly. Back in that underground facility in Saffron City, if not for Mewtwo's appearance, all those high-ranking officers would have already been captured by Dylan.

"Dylan, how do you plan to deal with it?"

Giovanni smiled faintly, his sharp gaze fixed on Dylan.

Mewtwo was his final trump card. He knew Dylan wouldn't fear it, but he was curious—how would Mewtwo compare to Dylan's other legendary Pokémon?

Especially under his command, as a top-tier Champion!

"Return, Marshadow." Dylan withdrew Marshadow.

Marshadow was strong, but it hadn't broken past its limits yet. Even though Mewtwo had only been awakened for less than a year, its power had already reached the level of a Tier 1 Legendary.

It would be too much to ask Marshadow to take on Mewtwo. There was no need for that.

Besides, since Giovanni was "breaking the rules" by using a Tier 1 Legendary, then he couldn't blame Dylan for doing the same! After all, it was Giovanni who "broke the taboo" first.

"As expected, if I'm going to fight Mewtwo, I have to use that."

Dylan thought it over and over again, but in the end, there was only one real choice. After all, the opponent was Mewtwo.

"Peppa."

Dylan opened his backpack and struggled to pull out a certain Pokémon that had been snoring away inside.

"…"

Mewtwo's psychic eyes immediately flared up with intense energy, locking onto the chubby, round-bellied Clefairy in Dylan's hands.

Giovanni narrowed his eyes slightly. Seeing it up close, this "Clefairy" was even more absurdly plump than he had imagined.

"Wake up. Wake up already."

Dylan slapped the pudgy cheeks of the so-called "Clefairy," only to find that it was sleeping as soundly as a dead log, completely unresponsive.

"Are you a pig? Get up and help me out already."

But it was useless. The atmosphere was starting to get awkward.

Dylan smacked it a few more times, but apparently, the thick layer of fat absorbed the impact, preventing his palm from getting through. The "Clefairy" remained in blissful slumber.

"Uh… Mewtwo, can you help me out here?"

Dylan hesitated before turning to Mewtwo for assistance.

"…" Mewtwo was silent.

The next moment, Mewtwo lifted its left arm, and an immense, invisible psychic force surged forth, its blue aura instantly enveloping the "Clefairy."

Slowly, it was lifted into the air, rising all the way to the ceiling. Then, with a sharp downward swing of Mewtwo's arm—

BOOM!

The "Clefairy" was slammed down at high speed, crashing heavily into the floor, sending up a thick cloud of dust.

"Miii! (Who the hell sneak-attacked me while I was sleeping?!)"

An enraged cry rang out as a pink psychic shockwave blasted outward, instantly dispersing the dust cloud.

The now-glowing, pink Clefairy floated up from the crater, its furious eyes locked onto Mewtwo. It was about to lose its temper!

Did no one understand how bad its morning temper was?!

"Mii?"

But then it saw Mewtwo and froze.

"Oh, it's you again, Mii. I don't wanna fight you. You always lose to me, and you still refuse to call me 'Mama.'"

Clefairy pouted, its earlier irritation vanishing in an instant. Although Mewtwo had been quite rude in waking it up, Clefairy still had a certain level of tolerance for it—after all, Mewtwo was technically its "offspring," created from its genetic material.

Clefairy wasn't the type to hold grudges anyway.

"…I DON'T EVEN HAVE A MOTHER!!"

Mewtwo's forehead visibly throbbed with frustration as its telepathic voice practically growled through clenched teeth.

Giovanni watched their exchange with great amusement. It seemed that something had happened during Mewtwo's previous encounter with Mew that he wasn't aware of.

But from the looks of it, Mewtwo had finally resolved its inner conflict regarding Mew. That was a good thing.

"Peppa, this is a proper battle. Your opponent is Mewtwo."

Dylan spoke to Clefairy.

"Eh? But it's my 'offspring,' Mii. I can't bring myself to hit it."

Clefairy's eyes sparkled mischievously, though it put on an exaggerated expression of reluctance.

Mewtwo's fists clenched tighter, its psychic aura flickering violently. What do you mean you "can't bring yourself to do it"?! You're practically grinning, you bastard!!

"Twenty crates."

"Teaching this kid a lesson is my responsibility, Mii!"

Clefairy agreed without hesitation. In an instant, it shimmered, reverting to its true form—the legendary pink Mew.

Giovanni's eyes widened in rare astonishment as he finally got a clear look at Mew's appearance.

Mewtwo, meanwhile, closed its eyes and repeated to itself: I have no connection to this thing. I have absolutely no relation to this pink pig.

"Dylan, this is really… Mew?"

Hearing Giovanni's question, Mew immediately turned and glared at him, puffing up in indignation. Was he… questioning its figure?!

"Peppa has been eating well since coming to my place, so please don't be surprised, Giovanni-senpai."

Dylan coughed lightly, giving a tactful explanation.

"…I see." Giovanni fell silent. He had nothing to say to that.

"However, a battle between Mew and Mewtwo isn't suited for an indoor gym. Giovanni-senpai, shall we move to the wild?"

Dylan glanced around. While Viridian Gym's battlefield was spacious, it was nowhere near large enough for a clash between Mew and Mewtwo.

Speaking of which, Galar's stadiums were much better in that regard—each one was built like a full-fledged arena.

"Fine." Giovanni nodded.

"Peppa, use Teleport to take us outside."

"Mii~"

Mew's eyes glowed with deep psychic energy. In an instant, the four of them vanished alongside Mew. Mewtwo's own eyes flickered, and it too disappeared from the gym.

In a clearing within Viridian Forest, Dylan and the others reappeared.

"This place should be suitable."

Dylan surveyed the open area and nodded in satisfaction.

"Viridian Forest, huh…"

Giovanni took in the fresh, natural scent of the forest, his tone carrying a trace of nostalgia. This was where his journey as a Pokémon Trainer had begun.

But he quickly adjusted his mood, loosening his tie and shoving one hand into his pocket as he turned to Dylan.

"Dylan, let's do this!"

Giovanni's voice carried an unmistakable edge of battle fervor. He was a calm, rational man, long past the age of reckless passion.

In the adult world, everything had a purpose—and for Giovanni, that was especially true. From the moment he took over Team Rocket, he had known he could never again be just a Trainer.

But this time was different. Mewtwo was his life's proudest creation, and its opponent was Mew!

"Ah~ Mew!"

Mew's body radiated pink energy, forming a protective barrier.

Mewtwo responded in kind, its blue psychic energy solidifying into a barrier of its own.

The next moment, both Pokémon ascended into the air and charged at each other.

BOOM!

Two powerful psychic forces collided, sending out shockwaves that rippled across the forest, causing fierce winds to roar in every direction.

Pink and blue light engulfed the sky, neither willing to yield.

Mew suddenly ramped up its psychic output. With a resounding boom, the rapidly expanding pink psychic energy forcibly pushed Mewtwo's blue energy away.

The overwhelming psychic power surged freely through the sky.

Mewtwo and Mew slowly retracted their external energy, facing each other midair in a tense standoff.

"Still at a disadvantage?"

Giovanni's eyes flickered slightly. He could tell that, at the last moment, Mewtwo had still been overpowered by Mew.

But he wasn't surprised. After all, Mewtwo hadn't been awakened for very long yet.

"As fellow Psychic-types, it's inevitable that a battle like this will take some time to determine a winner."

Dylan's lips curled into a faint smile—he had just thought of a rather interesting idea.

"That's true."

Giovanni nodded in agreement. But even if Mewtwo were to lose, it had to lose in style!

This was Team Rocket's ultimate creation, the masterpiece Giovanni had poured his heart and soul into. It was his proof, his pride!

"If that's the case, then let's do something different."

"?"

Giovanni narrowed his eyes slightly. Something different?

"Peppa, Imprison!"

"??!"

Dylan called out to Mew in the sky, and Giovanni's expression changed slightly.

Mew blinked in confusion. Imprison? What was that again?

It thought for a moment and then realized—oh, right, it did know that move.

Well, no helping it. Mew hadn't fought in who-knows-how-many millennia…

And it had learned way too many moves. Even when it did battle, it usually just brute-forced its way through with psychic power.

Mew's eyes gleamed, and a large, tangible "X" mark sank into its body—Imprison, a Psychic-type move.

A mysterious ripple spread outward invisibly.

Mewtwo suddenly felt something was wrong.

Below, Giovanni's brows furrowed tightly. Dylan… No wonder people secretly called him "vile" and "heretical." This wasn't without reason.

He actually had Mew use Imprison!

Then what moves was Mewtwo even supposed to use now?!

"Mewtwo, quickly test it—see which moves you can still use!"

Giovanni immediately spoke to Mewtwo via their telepathic link.

"…"

Mewtwo's psychic-blue eyes flickered wildly, then gradually dimmed, its gaze turning clear but bewildered.

"Gone…"

"What do you mean gone?" Giovanni was stunned.

"Dozens of my moves… all gone…" Mewtwo said blankly.

It raised a hand, staring at its own claws in near shock—its entire skillset had been wiped out…

"Not even one left?" Giovanni's brows knitted tightly. What kind of trick was that fat pink blob pulling?!

"Just Psystrike…"

Right, Psystrike was Mewtwo's signature move. It discovered that it could still use it.

But that was useless! Psystrike was a Psychic-type move. Using that?

It might as well telekinetically summon a spoon and smack Mew with it for some physical damage!

Giovanni: "…"

"No problem. Then just engage in close combat and rely on your superior melee skills to secure victory."

After a brief moment of contemplation, Giovanni made his decision—Mewtwo would go in for hand-to-hand combat.

That Mew was clearly a total couch potato. If not for its psychic power keeping it afloat, it probably couldn't even run properly.

Its melee combat skills had to be trash.

Meanwhile, Mewtwo was exceptionally skilled in close combat!

"Alright!"

Mewtwo's eyes reignited with psychic flames. It clapped its hands together and then pulled them apart—forming a massive, glowing psychic spoon.

Mewtwo gave it a stylish flourish, raising the spoon before it in a heroic stance—utterly fearless in the face of Mew.

Yes! Even if its moves were sealed, it would not fear this pink puffball!

"What incredible fighting spirit. In that case, Peppa, we have to take this seriously too."

Dylan smiled as he spoke. He could feel Mewtwo's unyielding determination.

As expected of Mewtwo—even stripped of all its moves, it showed no hesitation or despair. A worthy opponent indeed.

"Mew, Transform! Yveltal!"

"Got it, Mii~!"

Giovanni: ???

A dense, radiant light erupted from Mew, followed by a surge of chilling, red-black energy that spread wildly in all directions.

Mewtwo, still holding its spoon, felt its psychic senses go haywire—a deep, primal threat exploded in its mind.

"Mii~!!"

A piercing cry echoed through the sky.

Amid the swirling, malevolent black and red energy, a massive pair of dark crimson wings—shaped like sinister claws—unfurled.

A savage and terrifying presence descended upon the battlefield.

…Though, since it was Mew that had transformed, this Yveltal did look unusually plump.

But that did nothing to diminish the suffocating, oppressive aura it exuded.

"Whoa… What is that?!"

Lillie's heartbeat quickened as she stared at the fearsome black-and-red Pokémon.

It had the general form of a large bird, its outstretched wings and tail giving it the distinct silhouette of a giant "Y." Its body was covered in a haunting mix of black and crimson, radiating an eerie and ominous aura.

This was Yveltal, one of the legendary Pokémon known for its overwhelming presence.

There was no need to guess—just from its sheer presence alone, it was clear this Pokémon was immensely powerful.

"Rotom!"

Lillie's wide eyes sparkled with excitement. She had to know more about this Pokémon.

Even though this was just Mew's transformation, as long as Mew wasn't being careless, its transformations were perfect. Even a Pokédex would register it as the Pokémon it had turned into.

"Coming right up, Roto~!"

Rotom Dex immediately scanned the Pokémon.

Beep—Yveltal, Dark/Flying-type, the Destruction Pokémon.

Ability: Dark AuraMoves: Oblivion Wing, Brave Bird, Focus Blast, Sucker Punch, Phantom Force, Dragon Rush…

Description: When it spreads its wings and tail, glowing with red light, it drains the life force of living creatures. It is said that when its lifespan ends, it will absorb the life of all beings around it and return to its cocoon form.

"Wow!! It's so strong!"

Lillie's eyes shone with admiration.

This "Yveltal" was undoubtedly a top-tier legendary Pokémon—likely on the same level as Ho-Oh, Lugia, Reshiram, Zekrom, Solgaleo, and Lunala.

However, based on the Pokédex's description, it didn't seem to be a particularly benevolent legend…

And its aura was outright evil—so sinister and oppressive that it sent chills down her spine.

'Yveltal' slowly moved its massive, claw-like wings, staring down at the psychic-spoon-wielding Mewtwo.

Mewtwo, feeling the suffocating malevolence pouring off its opponent, lowered its gaze to its spoon.

Its psychic-covered eyes filled with doubt.

…Could it really win against that evil bird with this spoon?

"Come, Giovanni-senpai. Mewtwo. We're ready."

Dylan adjusted his collar gracefully and extended a hand toward Giovanni, signaling that he could make the first move.

Giovanni glanced at Mewtwo… then at the monstrous, terrifyingly oppressive Yveltal before them.

This…

Giovanni: (—)

Maybe… we should just forfeit?
